# 16b — Combat implementation plan

Draft 0.2, 2026-09-16, for [doc 16](16-combat-arena.md) Draft 0.2 on `main` at `5da8d6a`, revised after
the three-lens review in §10. Thirteen cuts, each with its steps, verify lenses and size, in the order
the dependencies allow. Sizes are approximate lines of production code plus tests.

---

## 0. Build discipline

1. Every cut ends with the workspace green: `cargo test --workspace --exclude mush-bot` plus
   `cargo test -p mush-bot` (the bot's exact-decimal JSON feature must not unify into the engine's test
   binaries), the contract gates blessed in the
   same commit that moves them (panic budget, state-slot owners, legacy-handler baseline, restriction
   lints census, module-namespace owners), all-target Clippy, and the story test and harness once they
   exist.
2. No second lane. A new capability extends the one that exists or replaces it in the same cut; nothing
   is kept "beside" for compatibility, because there is no data to be compatible with. The one shipped
   combat lane (`mush-sim::combat`) is retired by cut T (D-16-20).
3. No version ladders. A definition that changes is a new registry version with a migration chain; code
   carries one shape.
4. No fixture impersonates a production module. Tests compose the game crate's composition root, or drive
   `Arena::step` directly where no host is needed.
5. Each cut has a one-page design note in the ledger (§9) before it starts and an as-built entry when it
   lands, with the exact commands run. Reviews are performed by a reviewer that did not write the cut.
6. Panic policy: `panic → orderly exit`. The arena task's `catch_unwind` is the one place a panic is
   contained; it voids the match and commits nothing the faulted pulse touched, consistent with the
   world task's firewall.
7. Every new verb gets a non-baseline stanza in `crates/mush-contract-tests/legacy-handlers.toml` with a
   category and a note in the same commit; the pinned baseline only ever goes down.

### Load-sensitive test inventory (cut L, T-41)

The six suites first recorded as passing alone after failures in a loaded workspace run are
`mush-worldgen`'s `plan`, `review_plan`, and `s8_authority`; `mush-sim`'s
`journey::e7c6_b2`; and `mush-gateway`'s `two_process_e2e` and
`supervision_abandoned`. Later ledger entries also identify `mushd`'s `scripting_e2e`,
`mush-gateway`'s `server_panic_relaunch` and `restart_survival`, and `mushd`'s
`sim_reflex`. This is an investigation list, not permission to discard a failure: preserve the
first failure, establish its cause, and record both the whole-workspace and focused results.

The final cut L sweep also exposed an admission race in `mush-server`'s
`ipc_authoritative_action`: `tampered_offer_tag_is_rejected_before_intent` and
`authenticated_action_transfers_ownership_only_after_exact_intent` could receive `Busy`
before ownership. Their retry correction is recorded below; all eight tests in the
target passed focused validation at `02ead118` with normal test parallelism.
The Linux sweep at `5f8353b3` exposed the same pre-ownership race in
`actor_field_cannot_spoof_the_authenticated_connection` and
`a_peer_release_of_a_coordinator_action_cannot_kill_the_reader_loop`. It also
exposed socket-buffer assumptions in `mush-server`'s `ipc_teardown` and
`mushd`'s `load_soak`: both now observe detachment/timeout before draining.
The failures, fixture corrections and local/remote results are retained in the
[Cut L validation report](../reviews/cut-l-2026-09-22/validation.md).

Simulator integration waits in `mushd`'s `arena_story`, `arena_custody`, `observer_rule`,
`sim_cockpit`, `sim_lobby`, `sim_reflex`, `sim_rematch`, `sim_solo`, `sim_units`, and
`sim_weapons` use `tests/support::wait_until` for state transitions. Its deadline includes the
SSH or store read itself. Grace expiry waits for the named pilot's exact slot to read `left`
on the staff board, then asserts that reconnect does not restore cockpit control. The current
disconnect path releases focus and the arena binding but retains the human pilot's durable
billet until `EXIT` or check-in. The separate source-reviewed `B-81` in [16c](16c-simulator-backlog.md)
records explicit `+focus` and `STARTUP` admission after release: verified by reading at `8366b3fe`,
with no runtime probe and no established full control takeover; it is routed to cut B.4. Ledger checks
wait for the actual terminal event (`aborted` after `SIM ABORT`); movement premises use the
arena's reported clock.
Finite scripted firing sequences retain their explicit firing cadence and transient backoff.
The observer's provisional-phase tests load and select a test-owned sensor/unit pack with
`classify_after_ms = 15000`; the weapon, unit and rematch fixtures wait for the classified
callsign before retaining it across subsequent commands. The stock content stays unchanged.

---

## 2. Cut R: ruleset removal and base cleanup

**Goal.** One game, one sheet, one mechanic, one composition root; nothing that exists only to make a
second game possible.

### R-a (no deletion)

- R1. Hoist `mush-worldgen`'s structured-call machinery (`generate.rs` `generate_room` and
  `generate_brief`: forced tool, JSON-in-text fallback, repair round trip, typed validation,
  `prompt_hash`, priority, the `retryable | budget` error classification) into `mush-llm` as a generic
  `call_structured<T>`; re-point both worldgen sites to it. `ruleset-wod/src/st.rs`'s copy is not
  hoisted; it is deleted with its crate in R-b. `plan.rs`'s agentic loop is out of scope.
- R2. `git mv crates/ruleset-tacsim crates/tacsim`; add `composition.rs` and move into it:
  `server_modules`, `server_modules_with`, `tacsim_module`, `atlas_module`, `content_module`,
  `recovery_policy`, `run_module_migrations`, `SCHEMA_KEY`, `housing_title_issuer`,
  `market_title_issuer`, `org_capability_vocabulary` and `sim_routes.rs`; `mush-server/src/modules.rs`
  keeps host wiring and calls the game. Re-home `OnboardingConfig` into `mush-core`. Write down the
  new `tacsim` dependency list and add the `cargo tree -p tacsim` acyclicity test in the pattern of
  `mush-campaign` and `mush-industry`. Update `state_slot_owners.rs`'s `crate_family` (its
  `strip_prefix("ruleset_")` fallback) and every `ruleset_tacsim::` path in `config_ext.rs` (about 30).
- R3. `mush-ruleset` becomes a platform library: keep `schema`, `doc`, `store`, `chargen`, `render`,
  `vitals`, `interface`; delete the `Ruleset` trait's multi-implementation posture, `[rulesets].enabled`
  and `ruleset_modules`. `mush-tactics` keeps its `mush-ruleset` edge (it uses `pool_state`, `pool`,
  `vitals_frame`) and drops its dead `mush-dice` edge.

### R-b (the deletion)

- R4. The game sheet (`tacsim/src/sheet.rs`): `gunnery`, `piloting`, `sensors`, the character-first facts
  of doc 10 §17 to §19, the sheet verbs and chargen over the library. Size it against what
  `ruleset-wod/src/{schema, sheet_cmds, onboarding, state, roll, xp, agent, health, damage, chargen,
  chargen_cmds}.rs` provided (about 6k lines) and take only what the game needs now.
- R5. `mush-dice`: keep the expression evaluator for `+roll` and Rhai; delete `owod20` and `sr5`. The
  game's resolution (doc 16 §3.5) does not use it.
- R6. Economy and market: delete `SrCatalogSource`, `SrAvailability`, `SrDelivery`, `SrBoardObserver`,
  `vendor_caps`; give `housing_title_issuer` a game-owned id; rename `mush_housing::STORY_QUEUE` from
  `wod-story` to a neutral id and update `e5b.rs`; the `wod.usd` and `sr.nuyen` instruments go, the ten
  families arrive with cut C.
- R7. Delete `crates/ruleset-wod` and `crates/ruleset-shadowrun`; the `[wod]`, `[wod.economy]`,
  `[shadowrun]`, `[shadowrun.economy]` sections and their `Cfg` types; the WoD `ModuleAsync` arm in
  `world_task.rs`; `mush-market/src/record.rs`'s ruleset fields; `mush-npc`'s `wod_roll` and `sr_roll`
  abilities (one `check` ability over the game sheet replaces them) and the K8.4 providers
  `willpower_roll`, `willpower_set`, `condition_damage`, `condition_heal`.
- R8. Tests. Delete: `mushd/tests/{wod_e2e, wod_splats_e2e, wod_more_splats_e2e, shadowrun_e2e}.rs`
  and the two crates' suites. Re-point to the game sheet, never delete: the ruleset-specific cases in
  `mushd/tests/{intel_e2e, npc_e2e, onboarding_e2e, perception_e2e, persistence, power_use_e2e,
  web_e2e, modules_e2e, support/mod}.rs`, `mush-server/tests/{e7r2_proving_loop,
  h2c_ability_coordinator, market_issuer, production_action_migrations, boot_parity,
  provisioning_smoke, e4c_bridge, e6b_seal}.rs`, the four TUI goldens (`tui_sheet`, `tui_rolls`,
  `tui_damage`, `tui_vitals_two_feeds`) and the `mush-proto/tests/wire.rs` and `mush-core/src/lock.rs`
  fixtures. Rewrite as one-sheet tests: `mush-contract-tests/tests/{vitals_projection_clean,
  npc_ability_residual, legacy_handler_allowlist, state_slot_owners}.rs`.
- R9. Gates, in the same commit: remove the 49 ruleset stanzas from `legacy-handlers.toml` and lower
  `PINNED_BASELINE` accordingly; delete the `ruleset-tacsim` and `ruleset-wod` rows from
  `restriction_lints.rs` and correct its crate census; strip the `wod` and `shadowrun` writer rows and
  the two `jobs ->` edges from `module-ns-owners.toml`; delete `panic_budget/ruleset-wod.txt` and
  `panic_budget/ruleset-shadowrun.txt`; delete the `shadowrun_e2e` line from `.github/workflows/ci.yml`;
  re-bless the panic budget and state-slot gates.
- R10. Docs: README rows and superseded entries (done); docs 04, 08d, 08f, 08g and the example configs
  get a "retired, see 16 §11" banner (04 done) rather than edits.

**Verify lenses.** No workspace crate depends on a deleted crate; `grep -r "wod\|shadowrun\|nuyen"
crates/*/src` returns nothing outside history banners; `mush-ruleset` has one implementor; every test
that proved a platform property with WoD content still passes with the game sheet; `onboarding_e2e`,
`perception_e2e`, `persistence` are green.

**Size.** R-a about 2k lines moved and 1k added; R-b deletion about 63k (two crates 60.7k plus 2k of
`mushd` tests plus their fixtures), additions 6 to 10k.

## 5. Cut H: harness skeleton and baseline

  relief with roads, woods and water; 300 placements on the window; 12 fake sessions through

- H3. CI per D-H-8: a `tacsim-harness` job on `workflow_dispatch` and `schedule`, report-only, keeping

  in the same target behind its own flag.
- H4. The report: per-pulse p50, p95, p99, max in each tempo; memory at start and end; events, traces,
  cell probes, path queries and share operations per pulse; ledger bytes streamed.

## 6. Cut A: the arena core library

`crates/tacsim/src/arena/`:

- A1. `state.rs`: `Arena`, `ArenaDefinition`, `UnitState`, the epoch table, the shadow contact store, the
  due-event heap, the ledger egress ring.
- A2. `clock.rs`: tempo states, thresholds, hysteresis, `dt` clamping, `Lagged`, the substep length.
- A3. `intents.rs`: `Control` application, apply-time authorisation against the epoch table, continuous
  latest-wins, discrete sequenced and bounded.
- A4. `motion.rs`: doc 16 §3.4 per substep over `move_cost`; `index.rs`: the uniform grid.
- A5. `rng.rs`: `draw(seed, label)` and the enumerated labels; `hash.rs`: the per-substep state hash;
  `ledger.rs`: events, chunking, bounds, position summaries.
- A6. `step.rs`: the five phases of doc 16 §3.1 in order, each documented with its snapshot rule.
- A7. Tests: unit tests per module; the cross-tempo property test (one scripted intent and control log at
  5,000, 1,000 and 500 ms yields one event list and one hash sequence, crossing an impassable band and an
  occupied cell); a test that a tempo change mid-run changes no outcome; a test that adding a draw label
  shifts no existing draw.

**Verify lenses.** The arena module has no `use mush_store`, `tokio`, `GatewaySink` or `&mut World`;
`step` is `&mut self` over the inputs and nothing else; the harness at 300 moving units stays under
5 ms per pulse before perception and weapons exist.

**Size.** About 3.5k lines plus 2.5k tests.

---

## 7. Cut T: the arena task, the seam, custody, and the old lane's retirement

- T0. Disposition of the shipped lane (D-16-20): delete `mush-sim/src/combat.rs`'s verbs (`+fire
  +launch +heading +thrust +posture +dc +dock +eject`), the `tactical_intent` staging, the placeholder
  resolution in `tick.rs`, `s9_fire_gate.rs`, and the `+fire` stanza in `legacy-handlers.toml`
  (lowering the baseline). The `domaintick` pulse and `DomainTickCommit` remain for world-side
  non-combat domains; add a test that a `deployed` unit is never a participant of a world-side pulse.
- T1. `arena_task.rs`: owns an `Arena`, the inbox (`intents.max_pending_per_unit`), the egress ring
  (`ledger.max_unflushed_chunks`, the per-pilot frame slots), a timer driven by `next_due_ms`, a
  watchdog (`tempo.stall_ms`), `catch_unwind` around `step`, a `watch::Sender<bool>` stop signal, a
  `JoinHandle` on `PersistHandles` joined in both shutdown tails.
- T2. Custody. Check-out on `Boarding → Running` as one authoritative action: the `deployed { arena,
  snapshot }` marker on each unit record; refusals added with one shared helper at
  `TacticsDestroyGate::check`, `TacticsDetachGate::check`, `unit_damage`, `unit_remove` and the
  `@spatial` move commands; the world placement left frozen; `CustodyOut` sent. Check-in per unit,
  fallible: cells through `apply`, pose through `mush_sim::place`, `custody_lost` for a vanished unit;
  marker cleared; tank reset; seats, leases, focus and pods released. `Void` restores snapshots.
- T3. Routing: the cockpit verbs (cut K) validate focus, lease and epoch on the world task and forward
  `Intent`s; `Control` on seat activation, deactivation, revocation, linkdead onset and revival, `EXIT`,
  `SIM ABORT`; `Stop` on abort, timeout and void.
- T4. Output: the world task drains the arena's frame slots each iteration and emits them through its
  ordinary output path as `SendTarget::Char`; the arena has no `GatewaySink` and no session view.
  Ledger chunks are appended by the world task to `log:match.<id>` through `mush_core::Ledger` on its
  lane.
- T5. Boot reconciliation in the tactics recovery pass: `deployed` markers with no live arena are
  restored from snapshot, cleared, and their seats, leases, focus and pods released, with an
  `aborted(restart)` result.
- T6. `mush-contract-tests`: the arena library has no path to `ObjectStore`, `WriteBatch`,
  `GatewaySink` or `&mut World` (a source scan in the style of `restriction_lints`); the draw-label set
  is pinned; the custody markers are the only unit writes during a match.

**Verify lenses.** A `mushd` test and a gateway test board two fake pilots, start a match, drive, and end
by abort; unit records are byte-identical before check-out and after check-in when nothing happened,
and a unit driven ten cells has its world placement at the arena's final pose; a panic injected into
`step` voids the match with reason and the world keeps serving commands; a server restart mid-`Running`
leaves the units mutable and the pods free.

**Size.** About 3k lines plus 2k tests.

---

## 8. Cuts C, K, S, W, N, X

**Cut C, content and registry.**
- C0. The atlas `item` decoder gains `interior: bool`, applied as an interior space at pack apply; the
  eight pod items use it.
- C1. Typed decoders in `tacsim/src/definitions/` for units, weapons, ammunition, sensors, resolution
  tables and arenas; registration through the C1 registry as `RulesDefinition` entries.
- C2. `neighborhood.ooc_area.json` to version 4 in `mush-atlas`: `room_budget.maximum` raised to 10,
  the simulator lobby, red room and blue room, their exits, the selector item and the eight pods; the
  digest pin in `starter.rs` re-blessed. A test applies the upgrade on a world at version 3 and on a
  fresh world.
- C3. `content/arenas/proving_ground.json`: the 2.56 km arena with authored tiles, entry zones, key
  spots, the waypoint graph and the eight tank objects' initial placements; the tanks are created once
  by the arena pack's applier.
- C4. `content/resources.json`: the ten commodity families as `InstrumentDef` rows, registered at boot.
- Verify: the packs load on a fresh world and on a world at `ooc_area@3`; every definition has an exact
  version and validates its references.

**Cut K, cockpit and lobby.**
- K1. `match/` state machine with the named `Boarding → Running` trigger, `SIM` verbs, slots, `SIM
  START`, `SIM ABORT`, `match.max_duration_ms`, atomic release, the reset to `Idle`.
- K2. `BOARD` through `Transition::Place Interior`, the tank's station assignment,
  `request_seat_activation` with `helm`, `sensors`, `fire`, focus; `EXIT`.
- K3. Cockpit verbs as contextual selectors; `STARTUP` with the `power` capability and its due event;
  `VIEW`, `STATUS`, `WEAPONSTATUS`, `CONTACTS` frames.
- K4. Linkdead onset and revival delivered as `Control`; reclaim at a pulse boundary with a new epoch;
  the idle substitution recorded.
- Verify: two fake pilots complete `Selection → Boarding → Running → Aborted → Idle` twice in a row; a
  third account cannot claim a taken slot; a pilot who types `FIRE` then `EXIT` in one burst fires
  nothing; a pilot who disconnects mid-match reclaims on reconnect.

**Cut S, perception.**
- S1. Sensor and signature definitions; the pass (`arena/perception.rs`) as a due event over the index
  and the atlas trace with per-unit `Eye` heights; classification with the gap rule; team sharing.
- S2. The shadow contact store, id minting, the one refusal; contacts mirrored into the pilot's frame.
- S3. Notices with hysteresis; the world-side exclusion of the arena domain from `visible_rooms`,
  `units_for`, `+map`, `look` and unit listings; `unit.status` for deployed units.
- Verify: the observer rule surface by surface (cockpit, `+map`, `look`, `@unit/status`, notices, TUI,
  web); the harness's candidates, traces and probes per pulse.

**Cut W, weapons, resolution, damage.**
- W1. Weapon and ammunition definitions, `LOCK`, `FIRE` with cycle and ammunition, the percentage.
- W2. Resolution per doc 16 §3.5 over `direct_fire_path` and the silhouette tables; scheduled impacts as
  due events; revalidation; the blocked shot as a costed miss.
- W3. Damage as `UnitMutationPlan` through `apply`; consequences; wrecks; team elimination ending the
  match.
- W4. **The story test** in `mushd/tests/arena_story.rs` and its gateway twin.
- Verify: the story test; a shot at a target behind full cover costs a round and a cycle and misses; a
  moving target that leaves the predicted cell misses with reason; ammunition is conserved across a
  match; the money rule.

**Cut N, reflex AI and the cost run.**
- N1. The state machine controller and utility target choice in `arena/reflex.rs`; the waypoint graph;
  `[atlas].path_max_cells` raised for the arena; the `unreachable` edge; path queries bounded per pulse.
- N2. The harness drives all 300 units through it; `Engaged` p99 under 25 ms.
- N3. The deliberation hook: the order type, squad grouping, the decision pipeline request with a TTL,
  the doctrine fallback; the cost run mode behind `TACSIM_LLM_COST_RUN=1`.
- Verify: the harness thresholds; the cost run produces its table when the owner runs it.

**Cut X, extensions.** One cut per item of doc 16 §9, each adding its acceptance test and touching no
existing test.

What moved and why: F.2 journals `section_destroyed` on the transition only (51 to 38 at F.2); F.3 ends the two-teammate refresh loop and has every reader use the last-seen plot (`lost` 1,037 to 1,354, contacts at the end 1,090 to 727, and the fights themselves differ because the reflex controller no longer tracks through cover); every unit now carries a `SensorPass` due event. No X.1 to X.5 run was recorded, so this row is also their baseline; X.6 measures against it. |

| G | 2026-09-16 (this document §3; doc 16 §7 "Configuration"; §9 constants). R-b already deleted the ruleset config types, mappers, sections and the Shadowrun env (G1's first half, G2's deletions, G3's deletions), so G is the additive half. **Shape.** Five `deny_unknown_fields` sub-tables under `[tacsim]`, defaulted and parsed by `mush-config`, mapped by `config_ext.rs` into typed `tacsim::config` structs carried on `TacsimConfig`: `[tacsim.arena]` with its own sub-tables `[tacsim.arena.tempo]` (`quiet_ms` 5000, `alert_ms` 1000, `engaged_ms` 500, `alert_hold_ms` 30000, `engaged_hold_ms` 10000, `alert_range_cm` 0 = derived at match start as twice the longest weapon range in the match, `substep_max_ms` 250, `stall_ms` 2000), `max_catch_up_steps` 4, `[tacsim.arena.intents]` (`max_pending_per_unit` 8) and `[tacsim.arena.ledger]` (`summary_ms` 5000, `max_bytes` 67108864, `max_unflushed_chunks` 256); `[tacsim.match]` (`startup_ms` 10000, `board_timeout_ms` 120000, `max_duration_ms` 3600000, `max_slots_per_side` 12; the field is `match_` behind a serde rename because `match` is a keyword); `[tacsim.sensors]` (`pass_ms` 1000, `max_signature_factor` 1.5 as a TOML float, mapped to per-mille 1500 so the engine stays integer per doc 16 §3.3); `[tacsim.resolution]` (`paths`, the resolution tables of doc 16 §4, read and validated by cut W, parsed and inert here); `[tacsim.harness]` (`units` 300, `fighting` 24, `weapons` 150, `engaged_p99_ms` 25, `max_traces_per_pulse` 4000, `max_path_queries_per_pulse` 64, `max_ledger_bytes` 67108864). **Validation.** The `[tacsim.time]` precedent holds: a section nobody reads yet cannot refuse a boot on its values, so G validates nothing but shape — an unknown key in any of the five refuses the boot naming the section, which is the plan's "a typo in a new section refuses boot"; the cut that reads a field (A for arena, T for match, S for sensors, W for resolution, H for harness) validates its values in the same commit. **Surfaces.** The crate's key table in `config.rs` gains one row per key; `mushd.toml.example` (commented, so defaults resolve), `deploy/instance/mushd.toml.tmpl`, `deploy/dev1/mushd.toml` and `deploy/dev2/mushd.toml` carry every section with real values; `shipped_configs` `REQUIRED_SECTIONS` gains the five tables; `provisioning_smoke` renders and preflights them. **Tests.** `mush-config`: each section defaults and parses, each refuses one unknown key, the example and template parse; `config_ext`: the mapper carries every value and the per-mille conversion; nothing else. No verb, no module, no gate moves. About 600 lines. | The commit carrying this row, one commit: eight config structs, their typed twins, the mapper, the four shipped configs, `shipped_configs`, and the R-b key-table debt | Reviewed and re-verified at the workspace: `cargo fmt --all --check` clean, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean, `cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast` green on 337 binaries except the known load-sensitive four (`mush-worldgen` `plan`/`review_plan`/`s8_authority`, `mush-sim` `journey::e7c6_b2`), each green alone; `hex_vocabulary`, `restriction_lints`, `panic_budget` unmoved. Builder-verified before review: `cargo test -p mush-config` (96 + 5 + 5), `--test shipped_configs` (5), `cargo test -p mush-server --lib config_ext` (7), `--test provisioning_smoke` (4), `cargo test -p tacsim` (all targets), `cargo test -p mushd --test boot_composition` (6, which preflights `deploy/dev1/mushd.toml`), `cargo clippy -p mush-config -p tacsim -p mush-server --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo fmt --all --check`; the config tests prove each of the eight tables defaults to the constants, parses an explicit table, refuses one unknown key by name (and that `match_` is not accepted as a key), that the shipped example equals the defaults, and that the mapper round-trips every value and converts or refuses the signature factor. | (1) One file outside the note's list: `crates/tacsim/src/fixture.rs` holds an exhaustive `TacsimConfig` literal, so the five new fields were added there as defaults, and the literal stays exhaustive on purpose so a future block must be considered there too. (2) No crate-root re-exports for the new typed structs; `config_ext` names them as `tacsim::config::*`. Cut A adds the re-exports when it reads them. (3) The four `[tacsim.economy]` rows were missing from the config crate's key table since R-b; added here. (4) The signature-factor refusal is the conversion's own bound (non-finite, negative before rounding, or a per-mille past `u32::MAX`), not a sensor-policy ceiling: how far a signature may stretch a sensor's range is a value cut S reads and bounds. The final float-to-integer step is a guarded saturating cast, since `std` has no `TryFrom<f64>`. | K | 2026-09-16 (this document §8 "Cut K"; doc 16 §3.7, §3.8, §5; D-16-11). **Two green commits.** **K.1, the match lifecycle and the lobby (K1).** `tacsim::match_` grows from T.3's skeleton into doc 16 §3.8's machine: `Selection` (`SIM TEAM red\|blue` claims one slot per account, two to `match.max_slots_per_side` a side, no budgets or consent; `SIM LEAVE` releases it), `Boarding` (`SIM START` by a team lead or staff with at least one pilot a side; the pods lock to their pilots; the named `Boarding → Running` trigger fires when every claimed slot is seated or `match.board_timeout_ms` elapses with unseated slots dropped, an empty side aborting), `Running` (T.3's check-out and the arena; `match.max_duration_ms` ends it as a timeout), `Ended \| Aborted \| Void` (T.3's check-in, the result document, seats, leases, focus and pods released atomically, the reset to `Idle` idempotent and re-runnable by staff), `SIM ABORT` by any participant or staff, `SIM STATUS` anywhere in the centre. The lobby, red room and blue room and the eight pods are C.1's content; the `SIM` verbs are contextual to those rooms through a `CommandGate` that refuses them elsewhere with one line. T.3's `@arena/start` stays as the staff shortcut that runs `Selection → Boarding → Running` in one step. **K.2, the cockpit (K2, K3, K4).** `BOARD` (contextual in a team room for a pilot who holds a slot) places the character in the pod through `Transition::Place Interior`, assigns the tank's station to the character (the station record lives on the tank, doc 16 §3.3), activates the seat through `mush_sim::seat::request_seat_activation` with `helm`, `sensors` and `fire`, refreshes actor links and moves the account's focus to the station; `EXIT` reverses it and sends `Control { Released }`. The cockpit verbs are contextual selectors while focused on a station, each staging one intent through T.2's `forward_intent`: `STARTUP`, `HEADING <deg>`, `SPEED <kph>` (refusing "power not online" before the arena does, so the pilot gets the reason at once), `VIEW`, `CONTACTS`, `LOCK <contact>` (one byte-identical refusal until S), `FIRE [<weapon>]` (refused until W with the same line the arena would give), `WEAPONSTATUS`, `STATUS`. (8) `mush_atlas::trace::CellTrace` gains `target_cover`, which the walk computed and `trace_cells` dropped; one atlas test pins `trace_cells` and `direct_fire_path` agreeing over five classes. (10) One `state-slot-owners.toml` row for the money-rule test. Residuals: the acceptance trace (one per shot) and the lock-percentage trace (at most one per seated platform per pulse) are not counted in `PerceptionCounters.traces`; `target_gone` and `refused` are unreachable guards; `sight_condition` reads the first component carrying a `condition` cell; the harness's field-wide ammunition count is no longer a conservation law because a wreck's rounds burn with it, the exact law being tested on a fixture where nothing dies; `top` is carried and never selected. W.3: (1) Both story tests fight FRONTALLY (the tanks placed nose to nose 62 cells apart on the road, then driven to about 1,085 m), because a rear-quarter duel ends in `unit_disabled` (turret plus engine) rather than a wreck about one run in three, which doc 16 §3.8 allows but A1's "one tank is destroyed" does not; frontally the facet band reaches only the hull and the turret, a turret loss disables nothing, and elimination can only be the hull, at about twice the rounds. (2) The two pilots' accounts are seeded before the engine boots (as every sim lens does) because `mush_server::accounts_identity::hydrate` builds the character-to-account map once at boot and nothing updates it, so a player who registers after boot cannot claim a slot until the next restart; a live oracle needs the account id on `ServiceReq::CreatePlayer` plus a mutable `AccountMap` shared by `Engine::account_identity` and `mush_sim::AccountOracle`, a cut-sized change across three crates, not made. (3) The tests walk with `simulator` and `lobby` rather than the pack's `sim` exit alias, because the `SIM` lobby verb shadows the alias in the Welcome Hall and answers "The simulator is through the lobby." without moving the player (a content or verb-gate fix, not made). (6) The cockpit has no damage surface: doc 16 §5's "armor and internals per section as current over max" is not on the `Frame`, neither pilot is told of a hit, and the loser learns of the wreck from the centre's "Match 1 is over" line; the story asserts the destruction on the surfaces that carry it (the announcement, the hatch, the result row, `@unit/show`'s `section hull: wreck`) and the gap goes to the owner (a frame protocol addition). (7) The gateway twin carries no web-client witness: the split rig has a web listener but a second transport adds no observer surface the line client lacks. (8) The store-side claims (ledger order, result document, ammunition, money) stay with the in-process twin, as `arena_custody_split.rs` does. (9) One `state-slot-owners.toml` row for `arena_story.rs`; the gated census and `ci.yml` untouched (no per-lens job exists). |

(2) The dissipation carries its remainder in thousandths, because the literal `rate × dt_ms / 1000` is zero for any rate under four per second at a 250 ms substep and a tank at `Engaged` would never cool; the total over any interval is exactly `per_s × ms / 1000` however it is chopped (D-16-2), asserted at 500 and 2,000 ms pulses. (3) No `beam_cells` in `mush-atlas`: the beam walk over a `CellGrid` is `trace_cells`' walk, so the beam's attenuation is the trace's third field, weighted by the new `ErrorTerms.beam_attenuation` (default 1,000, the resolution pack unmoved). (4) `heat_per_shot` reaches the arena as a component cell and `HeatState` is read at check-out rather than carried on `CustodyUnit`, since six existing test files build `WeaponSummary` and `CustodyUnit` literally. (5) `FIRE` refuses at `heat >= heat_max` and the rise is capped at the cell's maximum, the ceiling being the cell's own max. (7) The acceptance destroys each leg the way `plan_hit` does (internals to floor, condition `destroyed`, one plan) and applies the authored consequences through the public `Arena::apply_consequence`, since no external caller can steer the facet draw to a leg. Note: the taller silhouette reaches the per-mille only through the width (doc 16 §3.5 step 1 takes the angular size off `width_cm`), so the biped is authored 5.2 m across the shoulders and the height is asserted as carried, not as arithmetic. **X.3:** (1) The power-and-speed term is authored on the sensor MOUNT (`ComponentRole::Sensor.thermal: Option<ThermalSignature>`) rather than as a column on `SensorDef.signature` rows, because `SensorDef`, `SignatureRow`, `SensorProfile` and `SignatureFactor` are built as exhaustive struct literals by eight existing test fixtures the cut may not edit; the type lives in `definitions/sensor.rs` beside the table and its comment records that moving it onto the row is a later cut. (2) `CustodyUnit`/`sensor_profile` do not carry it: the numbers ride the `TacticalUnit` as two cells laid by `bind` and are read at check-out, the `HeatState` route. (3) Two columns rather than one, `powered_permille` (online and stopped) and `cold_permille` (not online, including `Starting`), a target under way being 1,000. (4) A unit mounting several sights reads the first component in id order carrying the cells, mirroring `sensor_profile`'s first-sensor rule. (2) The reading is observer-inside: the bubble degrades what an observer standing inside the pod's radius can see, keyed on the observer's position once per pass (a target-keyed factor would need a scan per candidate), so a pod jams its own carrier, and a pod must be alive and `Power::Online` to jam (an emitter, unlike optics). (3) `ComponentRole::Ecm` rather than a second `Sensor` mount, so `sensor_profile`'s first-sensor rule is untouched. (6) `jam_checks` is additive and absent from the harness format strings; the cull radius is unchanged so the harness's perception line is identical. **X.5:** (1) `SensorDef.emission` became `Option<EmissionHook>` with an untagged pair `Eccm(EccmDef) \| Unread(serde_json::Value)` rather than a bare `Option<EccmDef>`, because a bare typed field moves the refusal into serde, whose message names the unknown key and not `emission`, and `tests/definitions.rs::a_filled_ecm_hook_refuses` asserts the message names `emission`; the untagged pair keeps the decode total, honours `deny_unknown_fields` inside the variant, and `SensorDef::check` refuses `Unread` by name. (6) The notes were trimmed to the decoder's 1,024-byte cap. **X.6:** One builder from a written brief, three lenses, a fixer, an independent gate (green first round, ten commands) and a judge who read the whole diff; one must-fix (the new `BlockedCause::TooSteep` and its sentence shipped untested; now pinned end to end and told apart from the impassable sentence) and thirteen should-fix, all fixed, no assertion weakened, no existing test edited (the one non-appended test-file line is an import gaining `Waypoint`). (1) The limit is SYMMETRIC where `physics::move_cost`'s is uphill-only: an uphill-only limit makes any bluff a one-way door into a cell a unit can never leave, with the reflex controller re-planning at its wall every `repath_ms`; the surcharge stays uphill-only. (2) The X row's 'slope entering `move_cost` through the atlas physics table' is met by `slope_pct` plus the table's `max_slope_pct`, NOT by calling `physics::move_cost`: its climb branch models a kerb inside one cell and the arena's cells are 10 m, so a legged unit stepping up 100 cm onto `Road` pays `cost * 110 / 100` here against the atlas's 150, pinned by an in-src test. (3) The diagonal constant still differs (181/128 against the atlas's 141/100; 565 against 564 at cost 400); it predates X.6 and is named, not fixed. (4) The X row's 'heightfield imported' and 'trace profile entering LOS' were already true since cuts S.1 and W; X.6 changes no LOS, perception, resolution, weapons or damage code and adds the edge only; hull-down is not adopted. (5) No content authored, against the row's 'each extension is a new definition version or a new content row': elevation needs no pack, version or pin, and `LINE_TANK_DIGEST` is asserted unmoved. (6) `z_cm` is set by the arena at check-out and the world's stored z is not maintained: `opening_pose`, `ArenaApplier::put` and `reset_stock_tanks` still write `z_cm: 0` so cut F.1's reset stays idempotent, and `same_pose` stops comparing z so a unit that never moved is not re-placed; doc 16 section 3.2's 'zero on the first arena' is thereby obsolete and is struck with this row. (7) A profile-less unit has no limit and still pays the surcharge (`None` is no limit, not zero), which keeps `entering_a_cell_carries_its_elevation` green unedited; `SlopeLimit::read` additionally falls back to a profile's first mode where `tracked_rules` falls to the stock tank, so a wheeled-only unit would drive on stock kinematics under its own 25 per cent limit, by intent, and no shipped unit is one. (8) `grade_pct` is hashed and `slope_limit_pct` is not: the grade has one writer, moves without an event and persists for many substeps; the limit never moves. (9) `effective_cost` uses `saturating_mul` where the brief wrote a bare product, which overflows `u64` at extreme inputs; identical on every reachable value. (10) `BlockedCause::TooSteep` was added (the addendum's preferred option; no existing test had to move) and is tested before `Occupied`, so a slope refusal never says anything about another unit. (11) The edge and shoulder rules were hoisted under `if let Some(limit)` by the fixer, a behaviour-neutral departure from the brief's literal line, so the unlimited lane the harness times does no refusal work. (12) The 12 ms gate is measured with `slope_limit: None`: every harness unit is `mobility: None` while every shipped unit carries `Some(45)`, so the harness delta is the surcharge alone and the six extra `blocked` are route drift, not slope refusals; test 10 is the only measurement of the limited lane (fewer nodes, about twice the wall-clock per search in debug); giving a harness cohort a profile is deferred to a non-X cut (owner question 4). (13) CARRY-FORWARD, a P1 against cut N.1's follower and not X.6: a unit that has wandered off its route drives at its goal in a straight line until `repath_ms` expires, and with slope in the world that line can now be a cliff; test 6's zero-`Blocked` arrival holds at `decide_ms` 250 / `repath_ms` 0 with the 10 m/s, 180 deg/s helm only, and test 9 records the stock-doctrine cost. (14) The short-substep truncation is left as it is: at `substep_max_ms` 150 the mover logs 5 `blocked` against 1 at 250 ms, printed and deliberately not asserted. (15) Fixture choices: test 9(a) uses a one-node waypoint graph (two nodes bounce the tank and it never rests); tests 3 to 5 read the steepest grade observed because `grade_pct` is the last edge crossed; test 8's units are blind and same-team so the event list is motion and routes only. (16) `line_cells` asymmetry: nothing anomalous across a full-width ridge on steep and shallow (2,1)-shaped pairs, both directions, no fixture tuning; the one-cell-crest case stays unexercised. (17) Legged units still price cells from the `tracked` row (X.2's simplification). (18) Deferred notes: a unit checked out onto a cell with no legal exit is stranded silently (unreachable on the shipped map, steepest edge 21 against 45; wants an `ArenaError` or a `from_pack` invariant if a steeper pack is authored); motion's diagonal slope branch and `shoulder_refuses` have no direct acceptance test; test 9(a)'s plateau check is end-state and 9(b)'s bound is doubled; the orphaned seat-release paragraph above `same_pose` and test 5's doc comment were corrected by the committer in this commit (comments only). **Found by the 2026-09-18 readiness review across X.1 to X.5 and not recorded when they landed:** (a) X.2 grew doc 16 section 7's closed consequence vocabulary from five kinds to six (`disable_motion_when_all_of`); (b) X.2, X.3, X.4 and X.5 added no cross-tempo test, which 16b section 11 makes mandatory for every cut that changes arena behaviour; X.6 carries its own and the four are still owed; (c) none of the extension units can be fielded in the simulator centre: the pods are bound to the arena pack's eight `line_tank` slots and no verb or config key selects a unit type; (d) doc 16 section 9 item 4 says ECM adds to the observer's `max_attenuation` budget where the code takes its cost off it (the source comment reconciles them; the amendment in doc 16 section 11 states it as built); (e) the reflex controller's `engage_orders` does not read the heat ceiling, so an overheated AI laser platform would stage a refused `FIRE` every tick (latent: every pod hands out a `line_tank`); (f) the opening pose's `z_cm` was zero on relief, which X.6 closes. |
| F | 2026-09-18 (the readiness review of the same date at `b1ec2c63`: seventeen read-only lenses over cuts R-a to X.5, every finding adversarially verified, one cargo runner, and a live smoke that booted the real `mushd` from a config file and fought three matches by hand over SSH; doc 16 §3.1 "Custody", §3.3 "The stock tanks are persistent objects", §3.4 step 4, §3.6, §3.7, §3.8, §5). **A cut the plan did not have: what stood between "A1 passes" and a person trying the centre by hand.** The slice was green and match 1 could be fought by hand; match 2 could not, and a pilot was told almost nothing. **Four green commits.** **F.1, the release half.** The eight stock tanks are reset to their `UnitSpec` and returned to their authored slot placement at every transition of the centre to `Idle` and at boot, never at check-in: check-in keeps committing the arena's final cells and pose, because that state is the match's evidence (A1 reads the wreck, the result document points at it) and the terminal centre shows it until somebody resets. The reset is keyed on the centre's roster, skips a unit already at spec, never touches a `deployed` unit or a unit staff fielded through `@arena/start`. One seat sweep (control released, seat released, billet vacated, focus released, body to the team room) runs from every pre-`Running` end, from `SIM LEAVE` by a seated pilot, from the reset BEFORE its idle early-return, from check-in and from the boot pass, so an abort during `Boarding` or a restart mid-match strands no billet and no body. A player's `SIM TEAM` on a terminal centre performs the reset and then claims, so two players can play again with no staff; `@arena/reset` stays. `EXIT` mid-match stays one-way (the N.1 amendment) but the arena drops the pilot from its frame roster on `Released`, the registry unbinds the pilot so a reconnect sends no `Live`, and the texts say so. **F.2, what a pilot is told.** Notices on the path that already carries the contact notices: the shooter's hit or miss with its reason and a kill; the victim's hit with the section and facet struck and nothing about the shooter; every arena-side refusal of an authorised intent (the broad form of the recorded double-`FIRE` drop), a purge staying silent; §3.4's `blocked` notice, one per stop. The frame gains §5's damage surface (armour and internals per section as current over max, conditions) and `STATUS` prints it. `section_destroyed` is journalled on the transition only. The hit percentage is the fired weapon's and is printed when the lock resolves. **F.3, what a platform knows.** A shared contact carries provenance: a platform shares only what its own sensors hold inside the time to live, and a share never raises a receiver's stamp above the sharer's own sighting, which ends the two-teammate refresh loop that made a contact immortal. A contact is reported at its last-seen pose with a true age, never at the target's live pose; the reflex controller reads the same. A viewer that runs no pass still expires what was shared to it. `CONTACTS` and the frame carry a range in metres rounded to 50 m beside the band. Every change is a due-event fact at an arena timestamp, so the cross-tempo property holds and is re-proved. **F.4, the front door.** The account oracle is a shared mutable map and `ServiceReq::CreatePlayer` carries the account, so a character made after boot can claim a slot with no restart (the recorded W.3 point). `deploy/dev1` and `deploy/dev2` turn the layer on; with it off `SIM START` names the real cause. The OOC pack's stale "not built yet" texts, the hall sign and the `sim` aliases that the `SIM` verb shadows are corrected at a new pack version with its pin; the `BOARD` and `SIM` gates stand aside outside the centre so the tour bus's `board` exit works again; the proving ground musters both sides on the ford road, in line and outside each other's sensor reach, at a new pack version. `deploy/README.md` says how an existing world takes the new OOC version. **Verify**: each sub-cut's own tests; A1 in both shapes; the observer rule; a rematch over real SSH with no staff that finds both tanks at spec and at muster; an abort during `Boarding` with a really boarded pilot followed by a different pilot boarding the same pod; a restart mid-match through the lobby leaving no body in a pod; the cross-tempo property with notices and the last-seen contact; a character created after boot claiming a slot in `mushd` and through the gateway; the workspace gates. About 3k lines plus 3k of tests. | **F.1** (`6feaa9f8`): **F.1, the release half** (uncommitted on `b1ec2c63`; about +1,770/-180 over 23 tracked files plus three new files totalling 2,120 lines). `crates/tacsim/src/match_/reset.rs` (new): `reset_stock_tanks(ctx) -> ResetReport { restored, remustered, deployed, failed }` walks the centre arena's authored `tanks[]` through the `tacsim:arena:<entry>` roster only, skips and reports a unit carrying a `deployed` marker, refuses a record whose `DefinitionRef` differs from the loaded binding, and otherwise applies `spec_plan(held, to_tactical_unit(spec, binding, object))` — one `UnitMutationPlan` of `SetCell`, `SetSectionCondition`, `SetComponentCondition`, `GrantCapability` and `RevokeCapability` with cause `arena:reset` (`RESET_CAUSE`), never touching structure — through the new public `mush_tactics::module::apply_plan` (hoisted out of `unit_damage` as C.2 hoisted `create_unit`: refuses a deployed unit, a non-unit, a frozen owner, a refused plan and an unencodable record, writes nothing on any of them, emits no panel frames; the L3 writer census moves `unit_damage` to `apply_plan`, still 7 sites), then re-places the tank on its authored cell with the applier's own arithmetic (`x*cell_cm + cell_cm/2`, `z_cm = 0`, `heading_mdeg`, the standing posture kept) through `mush_sim::place`; compare-first in both halves, so a clean world writes nothing and a second `@arena/reset` says nothing changed. Called from `lobby::reset` (the one funnel into `Idle`, now returning `ResetOutcome { was_idle, ejected, tanks }`) and from `boot_matches` itself, inside the boot pass, which no longer early-returns on an empty restored list. `arena/damage.rs`: `entry_consequences` / `EntryConsequences` read a record's `destroyed` and `wreck` sections at check-out with `apply_consequence`'s own rules, and `Arena::new` opens on those `MotionLimits`, drops the weapons of a disarmed or wrecked unit, gives a wreck no sensor, seeds `ammo` from `component/<weapon>/ammo` (falling back to `count_per_load`) and opens `disabled` for a unit with no weapons and no motion; `RemoveWeapons` and `destroy_unit` keep the `ammo` table so `returned_units` carries the true count home. One seat sweep in `match_/cockpit.rs`: `hand_seat_back` (`Control{Released}` on all three channels naming the pilot, then `SeatRequest::Release` or `Revoke`, then `vacate_station`), `eject_all -> EjectReport { bodies, seats }` replacing `disembark_all`, `eject_pilot`, `in_a_pod`; called from `lobby::release` for every pre-`Running` end, from `finish`'s `release_seat`, from `SIM LEAVE` by a seated pilot, from `lobby::reset` before the idle test, and at boot through `MatchBook::arm_boot_sweep` / `take_boot_sweep` performed once on the first `tacsim.board` tick (the pods resolve through the content latch, which is empty at tacsim's `on_world_loaded`). `SIM TEAM` on a terminal centre (`MatchState::is_terminal`) performs the same reset before the claim's own state test and announces it, worded off the report (a skipped or failed tank is said so in a second line); `SIM STATUS` on a terminal centre adds the `SIM TEAM red` or `SIM TEAM blue` hint; `@arena/reset` reports pods opened, billets given back, tanks restored and re-mustered, failures and deployed skips. `Control` gains `pilot: Option<ObjId>`; on `Released` the arena calls `Arena::drop_pilot(pilot, unit)` (pair-keyed, forgets the remembered frame, outside `state_hash`) and `send_control(ctx, unit, channel, state, pilot)` calls `ArenaRegistry::unbind_pilot` and `lobby::slot_left`, so `EXIT`, a `QUIT` and the linkdead grace running out all end the frames, send no `Live` on reconnect and render `, left` (new `Slot::left`, outranking `, seated`). Texts: `BOARD` says the panel wakes when every pilot is seated or in N second(s) and that leaving a running match abandons the tank; `EXIT` during `Running` says the tank is on its own and cannot be taken back; cockpit verbs between `BOARD` and the trigger answer `NOT_STARTED_YET`; motion and fire while `starting` answer `POWER_STARTING`; the three false staff-repair logs (`finish`, `roll_back`, the tactics boot pass) name the boot reconciliation; `finish`'s docstring says the tank is not reset at check-in. Gates re-blessed: `mush-tactics/tests/e7c11.rs` writer census, `state-slot-owners.toml` (two `AccountOracle` rows for the new test files), `module-ns-owners.toml` (the `tacsim -> tactics` delegation reason names `apply_plan`). Tests: `tacsim/tests/reset.rs` (11: wreck back at spec and at muster and a quiet second reset, the armed boot tick sweeps pods once and leaves tanks to the boot pass, a staff unit never reset, a deployed tank skipped, abort and board deadline and `SIM LEAVE` each followed by a different pilot boarding the same pod through the real `BOARD`, a terminal claim with no staff, the stuck-tank announce, the other selectors' guards, the idle-centre sweep), `tacsim/tests/cockpit.rs` (+4: the `EXIT` witness with unchanged `Channel::ALL` epochs across `Connected` and `Revived`, a `Disconnected` pilot shown `left`, the waiting panel, the starting refusal on its own 10 s window), `tacsim/tests/arena.rs` (the cross-tempo `Released` replay at 5,000/1,000/500 ms against a `Linkdead` reference), `arena/damage.rs` (+5 including `a_tank_wrecked_and_then_reset_can_be_wrecked_again`), `resolution.rs` (the destroyed tank checks in 12 rounds), `mushd/tests/sim_rematch.rs` (2 SSH: a rematch with no staff finding both tanks intact at 40/40 and at the authored eastings 65 m and 2,495 m, and a restart mid-`Running` through the lobby leaving no billet, no body in a pod, a 27/40 magazine and an off-cell tank put back by the boot reset, then a second match). The final gate after the repair is green on all nine commands including A1 in both shapes and the harness (engaged p99 6.55 ms). **F.2** (`ce236c7b`): **F.2** (`arena/{motion, state, perception, step, weapons, resolution, damage}.rs`, `match_/cockpit.rs`; no verb, config key, pack version, ledger event shape or gate file moved). `FrameNotice` is now an enum on the existing per-pilot road (`Arena::pending_notices` -> `Frame::notices` -> `arena_bridge::on_frames` -> `cockpit::notice_lines`, `Sys` lines tagged `arena`): `Contact{kind, contact, class}`, `Locked{contact}`, `Hit/Kill{contact: Option}`, `Miss{contact: Option, reason}`, `Struck{section, armor_section, facet, armor_left, armor_max, internals_left, internals_max}`, `SectionLost{section, consequences}`, `Refused{order, refusal, weapon, detail: RefusalDetail}`, `Blocked{cause: BlockedCause}`, with `kind()` and `contact()`; `NoticeKind` gains `locked, hit, miss, kill, struck, section_lost, refused, blocked`. The combat arms are staged from the substep's journal scan at the event's own arena timestamp. `Miss` and `UnitDestroyed` are attributed through the `Impact` that precedes them, so no ledger byte changes. The shooter reads `Hit on {contact}.`, `Miss on {contact}: {reason}.` (seven plain-word reasons, one per `damage::MISS_REASONS`) and `{contact} destroyed.`, or `Hit.`, `Miss: {reason}.` and `Target destroyed.` with no id when it no longer holds a contact. The victim reads `Hit on the {section}'s {facet} facet: {n} of {max} armour left.` or `... armour gone, {n} of {max} inside.`, and `Hit on the {section}, through the {armor_section}'s {facet} facet: ...` for a struck-through section; `Arena::struck_maxima` resolves the armour cell through the profile's `struck_through` exactly as `plan_hit` did. A lost section reads `The {section} is destroyed: {consequences}.`, built from the new pub `Arena::fired_consequences(target, section)`, which drops a `disable_motion_when_all_of` that has not fired while the ledger line keeps the authored list. Nothing about the shooter reaches the victim's line, and the type has no field for it. Pulse phase 4 no longer discards refusals: `Arena::stage_refusal` reads the cycle remainder or the heat at the refusal, and `cockpit::refusal_line` renders `{ORDER} refused. ` followed by the same `CockpitError` string the local pre-check prints, plus `Your tank is a wreck.` and `That tank is no longer in the match.`. A purged intent and the reflex controller's direct `resolve_fire` stage nothing. `Ok` from `resolve_lock` stages `Locked`, rendered `Locked on {contact}, {N}% to hit.` only while the frame's lock is still that contact and bare otherwise. The `LOCK` verb's own W.2 echo is deleted, so the arena's notice is the one owner of that sentence. Motion: `BlockedCause{Impassable, Occupied, Edge}` on `MotionOutcome.blocked_by`. `integrate` is split into `advance` plus one exit that settles the new output-only `UnitState::held`: set by a stop, cleared by a substep that covered ground or by the speed order coming off, not in `state_hash`, never read by the reflex controller. The pilot's `Held: ...` line (three sentences, the occupant never named) is staged in the motion loop on `held`'s rising edge, and `Frame::blocked` reads `held`, so `STATUS` says `HELD` through the zero-travel pulse remainder. The frame gains `sections: Vec<FrameSection{id, condition, internals: Option<(cur, max)>, facets: Vec<FrameFacet{id, armor, armor_max}>}>` from the pub `Arena::frame_sections`, in section-id then facet-id order, built per seated pilot only and compared in `Frame::same_as`. `STATUS` prints it through the pub `cockpit::section_lines`, two lines per section wrapped at 76 columns. `status_frame` gains `sections`, `heat`, per-weapon `p_permille` and `percent`, and a nullable `notices[].contact`, with still no `shape`. Damage: `Struck.fresh` gates `section_destroyed` to the transition. A round on a spent section is a `hit` that finds nothing left to break, not spilled to the hull, and its empty-op plan is not applied, so no revision bump. Hit chance: `Arena::lock_aim -> Option<LockAim{target, range_cm, cover}>` replaces `Arena::lock_p_permille`, `Frame::lock_p_permille` is removed, and `frame_weapons(platform, Option<&LockAim>)` resolves `FrameWeapon::p_permille` per weapon through `resolve_shot` on one trace. `WeaponRow::render` appends ` {N}% to hit`, and `lock_line` and the payload's `lock_percent` quote the weapon a bare `FIRE` fires (`DEFAULT_WEAPON`, else the first). Tests: `tests/resolution.rs` 15 -> 29, covering shooter and victim notices with a line-by-line no-shooter check, miss reason, kill then silence, `section_destroyed` once, six refusals, the two silences, the chance on first `LOCK`, two weapons with two chances, two locks in one pulse, the struck-through sentence, the status block pinned against its cells inside 80 columns, and three cross-tempo runs (the duel, a drive into a bank with two refusals, a spent section) comparing notices, events and hashes at 5,000/1,000/500 ms. `tests/arena.rs` gains 2: one `Held:` line per stop for each of the three causes, and `held` against `blocked` on a 1,030 ms pulse with `frame.blocked` asserted. `tests/extensions.rs` pins `fired_consequences` on one leg and on two. `mushd/tests/sim_weapons.rs` over real SSH: one `Locked on` line per repeat LOCK, the outcome line waited from a mark, a coaxial burst for the victim's half, and `STATUS` moving. `observer_rule.rs`, `arena_story.rs` and `mush-gateway/tests/arena_story_split.rs`: the leak lists are widened, Bo is told of every hit and of its hull with nothing of Ada line by line, and Ada is never shown `Hit on the `. Harness under `TACSIM_ASSERT=1`: `Engaged` p99 7.0 to 7.6 ms against the 25 ms gate. `ArenaTerrain::cell_at` and `index::range_between` so a plot's range and cell are the pose's own arithmetic; `hash.rs` folds both plots and the own stamp through one `fold_plot`. `arena/perception.rs`: a detection writes stamp, plot and own sighting together off the pass's own snapshot; `share_pass` shares only a classified contact the sharer's own sensors saw inside the time to live, hands over that sighting's own stamp and own plot (never `seen_at`, which may be a teammate's), updates a receiver only when the stamp is newer and never to `t_ms`, and inserts a mesh copy with `first = last =` the sighting's stamp and `own_seen: None`; `sensor_pass` no longer early-returns, so the loss band runs at every platform's pass and its return value is `shareable`; `schedule_first_passes` and `step`'s reschedule cover every unit in the match; `contact_within_alert_range` gains the wreck filter and reads the plot. `arena/step.rs`: `frame_contacts` reads bearing, range and cell off the plot and `age_ms = now - last_seen_ms`; `Frame::weapon_reach_cm` is deleted. `arena/reflex.rs`: `hostile_in_weapons_range`, `best_hostile` (range and `facet_band_seen`), the Approach and Engage ranges, `stand_off_cell(&SeenPose)`, the engage heading and `engage_orders` all read the plot through one `plot_of`. `arena/resolution.rs`: `lock_aim` resolves range and cover off the plot (`LockAim` gains `seen_at`), and the advertised chance has its own path `Arena::resolve_seen` (band from the plot's heading, crossing term from the plot's speed, a beam's attenuation to the plot's cell via `damage::fire_attenuation_at`), sharing `cone_urad` and `presented` with `resolve_shot`, which stays on the live target for `FIRE` and the impact; `damage.rs` gains `fire_cover_at`/`fire_attenuation_at` over one `fire_trace_at`. `arena/weapons.rs`: `DEFAULT_WEAPON` moved here (re-exported by the cockpit), `UnitState::default_weapon`, `UnitState::fire_bands_cm`, `WeaponSummary::bands_cm` (one band at `max_range_cm` for a bandless weapon), `FrameWeapon::bands_cm`, `frame_weapons` on `resolve_seen`. `arena/command.rs` and `deliberation/{order, request}.rs`: the squad brief reads the commander's plot, `SquadSignal.reach_cm` becomes `bands_cm`, `RangeBand::ceiling_cm(&[u32])` is first/3, first, last. `match_/cockpit.rs`: `range_band(range_cm, &[u32])` against the fired weapon's authored edges, `CONTACT_RANGE_STEP_M = 50` and `range_m_rounded`, `ContactRow::range_m` printed as `<band> <N> m <age> s`, `status_frame` contacts gain `range_m`, `refusal_line` matches all fifteen `Refusal` variants by name with six new sentences and `Overheated: let it cool.`, the `CONTACTS` help line. No new verb, config key, pack version, draw label or content change; no golden hash exists in the tree, so nothing was re-blessed. **F.4** (the commit carrying this row): **The account map learns.** `mush_server::accounts_identity::AccountMap` is now a `std::sync::RwLock<BTreeMap<ObjId, AccountId>>` behind the one `Arc<dyn AccountIdentity>` that `build_engine` installs into `Engine::set_account_identity` and into the `mush_sim::AccountOracle` slot. `mush_core::AccountIdentity` gains `link` and `unlink`, whose default bodies answer `false`. `AccountMap::link` is idempotent and refuses to move a character to a second account. A poisoned lock stops the learning, keeps the answers, and logs once. `Engine::link_account_character` and `Engine::unlink_account_character` delegate to the installed oracle. **The wire.** `mush_ipc::IPC_VERSION` is 6. `ServiceReq::CreatePlayer { name, account: Option<AccountId> }` carries the account. The world task's `CreatePlayer` arm calls `link_created_character` before `ObjCreated` and before the reply leaves. `run_newchar` passes `account_id_of(&account)`, the same `(name, created)` mint `hydrate` uses. The compensating `ServiceReq::UnlinkPlayer { character }`, `ServiceResp::UnlinkPlayer(bool)`, `WorldMsg::UnlinkPlayer` (classed `WorldRead`) and `WorldHandle::unlink_player` are sent from both failed `store.link_character` arms: `Accounts::create_and_link` through `forget_character`, and `run_newchar`. `mush_sim::on_obj_destroyed` unlinks a destroyed character through `AccountOracle::unlink`. `SimError::UnknownAccount` now reads "This character has no account on record, so the simulator cannot hold a slot for it." **The layer switch.** `deploy/dev1` and `deploy/dev2` write `[tacsim]` with `enabled = true` ahead of the dotted blocks. `mushd.toml.example`, `mushd.toml.tmpl` and `instance.env.example` say that the flag instantiates the simulator. `lobby::require_layer` is the first line of `claim`, `leave`, `start`, `abort`, `status_lines` and `cockpit::board`, and answers the new `SimError::LayerOff`. `SimError::NoUnit` names the boot log, not a re-apply that no verb performs. The boot logs `tacsim enabled: …` or `tacsim disabled (…)`, then `simulator centre ready … roster_tanks=N` or one of two `NOT ready` warnings, through the new `tacsim::match_::centre_readiness`. `shipped_configs.rs` gains `tacsim.arena.reflex` in `REQUIRED_SECTIONS` and parses both dev boxes. **Content.** `neighborhood.ooc_area` is at version 5, digest `0218b87b4429dc60a4657d608f1446f87fe60e88df2871bd31f2bed39c67eb42`. The selector, its screen and buttons, and the eight pods and their panels say what is true; the pod description is state-neutral for a pilot sealed inside a running match. The hall sign points at `simulator`. `sim` is off the hall exit (`Simulator Lobby <SIMULATOR>;simulator;annexe`) and off both team-room return exits (`Simulator Lobby <LOBBY>;lobby;out;…`). `neighborhood.staff_lobby` stays at version 3, requires `ooc_area@5`, and has digest `fd86e03b014ac80299ce502c0eb063b0dd75b993730f2504d71d9c3d0f1c33ef`. `SimGate` is deleted: `sim_command` refuses `SIM STATUS` outside the centre through `lobby::outside_centre`, and the other four selectors refuse in their own handlers. `CockpitGate` tests the verb first and stands aside when `where_in_centre` is `None`, so `board` at the Bus Stop boards the tour bus again. `tacsim.pack.proving_ground` is at version 2, digest `0ecc97a0922b2fef3d18fd2b510c2a17cdef1613de10ee56b9fce99520924160`. Slots 1 to 4 stand on rows 97, 95, 99 and 93 at columns 6 and 249 (as left by the residuals pass; F.4 first took 97, 93, 101 and 89), all inside the ford band, with headings unchanged. `muster_red` and `muster_blue` are on row 105 with their edges kept. The atlas terrain pack is unchanged. F.1's `reset_stock_tanks` reads the compiled definition, so an existing world remusters at its next boot. **Help and deploy.** `docs/help/chargen.md` is rewritten for the pilot sheet: three ratings, six points above a free 1, cap 5, the `pilot-chargen` queue, and gunnery as the only rating read. `docs/help/simulator.md` and `docs/help/cockpit.md` are new, and `getting-started.md` points at them. The `WEAPONSTATUS`, `STATUS` and `@arena/reset` help lines are completed. `deploy/README.md` gains "The simulator centre": the one key, a fresh store as the supported path, the ordered `@atlas/pack plan` and `apply` upgrade for `ooc_area` then `staff_lobby`, the arena pack being off the shelf, F.1's boot remuster, and the `accounts.json` first staff login. **Tests.** `mushd/tests/front_door.rs` has two cases on an empty store over SSH: two welcome-screen accounts each claim a slot, and an `@newchar` alt is refused a second slot. `mush-gateway/tests/front_door_split.rs` is its twin through the split. `accounts_identity` adds four unit tests. `world_task` adds `f4_the_account_map_learns_on_create_and_forgets_on_unlink`. `mush-sim/tests/e7c5.rs` covers the destroy observer. `boot_parity.rs` asserts the boot lines in both layer states. `match_lifecycle.rs` covers layer-off refusals for all five selectors and `BOARD`. `sim_cockpit.rs` covers the gates: `board` at the Bus Stop, the ten other verbs falling through to the handler's sentence, a bare `SIM` in the Red Room, and `look Simulator Selector`. `interior.rs` covers version 4 to 5 through plan and apply with the hall sign included, a fresh world, and the fixture-tracks-version guard. `content.rs` holds the muster invariants, read from the pack. `gateway_link_parity` sweeps ten variants. `state-slot-owners.toml` gains one row for `front_door.rs` (census 38). No verb, config key, draw label or frame field was added, and nothing under `arena/` changed. **The residuals pass** (same commit as F.4, from the F.4 judge's notes): the three gateway split rigs (`arena_story_split`, `arena_custody_split`, `front_door_split`) retry their first login for about five seconds while the server half answers `the game is shutting down`, because the rig awaits only the gateway's `ssh_ready` and nothing waits for world admission; `docs/help/cockpit.md` says truthfully that only `STARTUP`, `LOCK` and `FIRE` are answered with the `{ORDER} refused.` prefix; the comments that still described the deleted `SimGate` are corrected and `front_door.rs` installs the oracle before `boot_modules`, which is production's order; the `not mapped yet; reconnect` advice is gone from `mush-campaign`'s three seat refusals (`NO_ACCOUNT_ON_RECORD`) and from `mush-sim`'s `+focus` refusal; `sim_command` checks the location before it parses, so every spelling of `SIM` outside the centre gets the one `OUTSIDE_CENTRE` sentence; `board_deadline` returns at once with the layer off; the proving ground's slots 2 to 4 moved inside the ford band (rows 97, 95, 99, 93 at columns 6 and 249, the pack still version 2, digest `0ecc97a0…`), and the muster test finds every slot by id and asserts every slot's straight run is passable from column 6 to 249; `mush-ipc`'s roundtrip samples `UnlinkPlayer` both ways. | Each sub-cut: one builder from a written brief, three independent lenses on the uncommitted diff (correctness, conformance, tests), a fixer who fixed or rebutted every must-fix and should-fix finding, a gate run by an agent that wrote none of it, a judge who read the whole diff, and the committer reading the gate's own log files. (2) `held` is a second, output-only condition beside `blocked` rather than a redefinition of `blocked`. (4) `SeenPose` is five integers and not a `Pose` clone (a pose carries the driver's opaque posture and the store is `Copy`), and it carries `speed_cms` beyond the brief so the advertised cone's crossing term has a plot-side input and the advertised and drawn chance agree while a target is in continuous view; it is hashed with the rest of the plot. (15) The final gate ran nine commands and both harness tests green but no full-workspace sweep on the final tree. **F.4.** (1) All five `SIM` selectors and `BOARD` refuse with `SimError::LayerOff`, the read `SIM STATUS` included. The brief names only the subcommands that claim, start or board, and the design note says `SIM START` names the cause. A healthy-looking board over a server with no arena is the defect the finding describes. (2) `SimGate` is deleted outright, not given a stand-aside arm, and its `match_::SimGate` re-export is gone. With the stand-aside it would decide nothing, and `sim_command` gives the same `OUTSIDE_CENTRE` line from the command table, where exit and player aliases get their turn first. As a consequence, `sim wibble` and a bare `SIM TEAM` outside the centre now print usage and "Which side?" before any location check. (3) The shared map is reached through two default-`false` mutators on the `AccountIdentity` trait and a `std::sync::RwLock`. The brief suggested "no lock". Both readers already hold the one `Arc<dyn AccountIdentity>`, `&self` on a shared `Arc` needs interior mutability, and a fixed test oracle now refuses a write it would otherwise drop. (4) `account` is an `Option` on the wire. `None` means the id could not be minted; the character then stays unmapped and fails closed, and both halves log it. This avoids turning an unreachable mint failure into a hard account-creation failure. (5) A second wire variant at the same `IPC_VERSION` 6, `ServiceReq::UnlinkPlayer` with `ServiceResp::UnlinkPlayer`, was added by the fixer. Without it the RAM insert would outlive a failed `store.link_character`. It is classed `IngressClass::WorldRead`, because it touches the oracle only and can only make a policy fail closed. If it is refused mid-commit, the mapping stands until the next boot, which is logged. No staff relink verb exists, so none was covered. The legacy `accounts.json` import needed nothing, because it runs before `hydrate`. (6) A poisoned map lock keeps answering reads from the intact table and refuses both writers. An empty table would silently unmap every character on the server. (7) The readiness line is logged from `mush-server/src/boot.rs` after `boot_modules`, through the new `centre::readiness`. It is not logged from tacsim's own `on_world_loaded`, because the content module composes after tacsim, so a first boot has no rooms or roster there yet. (8) The muster rows were taken exactly as suggested: 97, 93, 101 and 89. Slot 4's row 89 is therefore outside the ford band (rows 92 to 102), and its straight run still meets deep water. Only slot 1's run is asserted. The muster nodes went to row 105. (9) The arena pack's `note` was rewritten, not appended to, because `ArenaDef.note` is capped at 1,024 bytes. (10) There are two help files, `simulator.md` and `cockpit.md`, because topics key on the file stem with no alias mechanism. `help simulator` names `look Simulator Selector`, not a detail keyword: pack details are written as `atlas:detail:*`, which `look` never reads, and the selector carries no alias. `cockpit.md`'s refusal example for `HEADING` carries the `refused.` prefix, which only `STARTUP`, `LOCK` and `FIRE` print. It is left as a one-line help fix. (11) Files outside the brief's list, each given one truth fix: `deploy/instance/instance.env.example`, `docs/help/getting-started.md`, the `@arena/reset` help string F.1 left incomplete, and the callsign doc comments in `sheet/chargen.rs` and `sheet/schema.rs`. (12) The dev-box config test holds `deploy/dev1` and `deploy/dev2` to the `[tacsim*]` family only, excluding `tacsim.recovery` and `tacsim.economy`, since the other required sections are deliberately absent from those boxes. (13) Deferred and recorded, none of them in doc 16's wording: the three `not mapped yet; reconnect` refusals in `mush-campaign/src/seatcmd.rs` and the one in `mush-sim/src/commands.rs`; `docs/help/wod.md` and `shadowrun.md`; the `l` alias on five exits shadowing `look`; `board_deadline` ungated on a layer-off centre; the stale gate comments at `sim.rs:244` and `lobby.rs:57`, and the stale "eleven" at `shipped_configs.rs:36`; no scripted first staff login; no `mush-ipc` roundtrip sample for `UnlinkPlayer`; and the split rigs' unguarded boundary-readiness window, which failed `arena_story_split` once in the gate (it passed alone and in a full-package rerun). Doc 16 specifies neither the `SIM` gate nor the muster rows, and §3.7's verb set is unchanged. **The residuals pass.** (1) It supersedes F.4's deviation 8: the muster rows are 97, 95, 99 and 93, all inside the ford band, where F.4 first took 97, 93, 101 and 89 and left slot 4's run ending at deep water. (2) It closes most of F.4's deviation 13 (the seat and `+focus` refusals, the stale gate comments, the ungated `board_deadline`, the `UnlinkPlayer` roundtrip sample, the split rigs' readiness window). The `board_deadline` gate is hardening, not a fix: the match book is RAM and every route into `Selection` or `Boarding` already asks for the layer, so a non-idle centre on a layer-off server is unreachable, and its test reaches the state by replacing the `TacsimStore` slot under a live centre. Only the deadline is gated, never the whole tick, because the tick also performs F.1's one-per-boot pod sweep. (3) One existing assertion changed: `mush-sim/tests/e7c5.rs` now expects `no account on record`. (4) Still open after cut F, by decision or for the owner: the help topics of the two deleted rulesets still ship; five shipped exits carry the alias `l`, which shadows `look`; pack details are written as `atlas:detail:*`, which `look` never reads; a fresh store has no scripted first staff login (the documented route is the legacy `accounts.json` import with a hand-made argon2id hash); `help simulator` and `help cockpit` are not asserted reachable; no test drives a failing `store.link_character` through `create_and_link`; the muster constants in three mushd lenses are not tied to the pack. (5) Not in cut F at all, recorded by the review as the next candidates: the reflex A* ignores occupancy, so an AI tank queues for ever behind a parked unit or a wreck; `Withdraw` is terminal; one human cannot start a match against AI fill; the TUI and the web client render nothing for `VIEW`; motion truncates on short substeps; no arena, match or harness config value is validated; the extension units cannot be fielded in the centre. |
