The bracket prints during `Selection` only, because `SIM STATUS` answers in every state and from inside the pods, and a type on the board mid-match would hand a seated pilot the chassis of a contact their own sight still renders `X1`. The observer rule won over the brief's illustration, and the brief's own test item 4 required it. (2) `build_game` is not byte-identical for one input. `build_from`'s dedupe is digest-aware, so an operator pack wearing a shipped `(id, version)` under a different digest is refused rather than silently skipped, on `build_game` as well as `build_fielded`. The brief itself offered this rule ('refused only if the bytes differ'), and C.2's justifying comment cited a `verify_sources` check that never existed. (3) `lobby::leave` runs `reset_stock_tanks` on the emptying release. The brief did not ask for this. Without it, a refused half-refitted `SIM START` followed by everyone leaving handed the next pair a biped, and `refit_chosen_units`' no-rollback rule rested on a false invariant. (4) The fieldable list is in canonical-id order, not the brief's illustrative order. It is the only deterministic order a `BTreeMap` offers, and it is pinned verbatim. (5) The bare `SIM UNIT` also needs a slot, because the sentence reports `Yours:`. A bystander is told to claim first, even in states where claiming is refused; this is left as a note. (6) The brief's statement of known limit 2 is wrong for `line_tank_laser`. That platform carries no cannon, so `choose_weapon`'s first-weapon fallback fires the beam on a bare `FIRE`. The help states the real rule. (7) `@arena/reset`'s tank line gained `, {n} back to their own type`, which the brief did not list. Without it, a pass that rewrote documents would print zero and zero; the line is byte-identical when nothing was refitted. (8) `reset_one`'s old `re-apply the arena pack` refusal is deleted rather than kept beside the refit. A refit handles a version mismatch too, and 16b section 0.2 forbids a second lane. (9) `refit_unit` writes through the governed `persist_unit_document`, not `create_unit`'s raw outbox, and uses `replace_active`, so the lifecycle row and custody marker are kept. (10) The held-billet leg of the `refit_unit` test is proved structurally: a one-op write census plus whole-`SpatialState` equality, with no seat granted. No production path refits a seated unit. (11) One census outside the brief's list moved: `e7c11.rs`'s `UnitStore` writer pin went from seven to eight sites. (12) Deferred and named. A slot cleared to the authored type, or re-claimed, after a refused half-refitted start still fields the refitted tank with no bracket. With `ai_fill` on, a pod whose pilot chose a type and then left or timed out during `Boarding` is filled with the refitted tank. `SimError::Refit` quotes a raw `#N`. The legged cell-cost, `DEFAULT_WEAPON` and AI-fill-type limits are stated, not fixed. `sim_reflex.rs`'s load flake is pre-existing. (13) Doc 16 amendments owed, none of them edited here. Section 3.3: a pod's tank may be refitted for one match, and every return to `Idle`, the last `SIM LEAVE` included, puts the authored type back. Section 5: the board bracket and the `SIM UNIT` announce publish both sides' choices during `Selection` only. Section 9: the five items join the fielded catalog, which closes X.1 deviation 1's open question as yes for the fielded game and no for `build_game`. **The closeout pass.** (1) It supersedes Q.8's deviation 5 (the no-slot advice) and deviation 12's first four items (the help's laser line, a cleared or re-claimed slot fielding the refitted tank, the AI fill taking a leaver's refit, `SimError::Refit` quoting a raw object number), and Q.6's deviations 8 and 9 (the unused derives, the two stale five-against-one citations, the three loose carry comments, the unresolved `SIN_ONE` link). (2) Two choices of its own, both said in the doc comments: an UNCLAIMED pod whose tank cannot be put back is logged and skipped where a claimed slot refuses the start, and a non-last `SIM LEAVE` un-fits only the leaver's own pod and never the whole roster. (3) Every doc 16 amendment the judges listed as owed is written in the commit carrying this row. The historical rows of this ledger are NOT rewritten, by decision: the cut A row still prints the old substep quotient, the cut X row still says the mesh is deliberately not jammed and that four cross-tempo tests are owed, and the T, K and S rows still say `tacsim.frame`. They record what was true when they landed; this row and doc 16 carry what is true now. **Still open after cut Q.** (1) Rulings wanted from the owner. (a) The ledger's `blocked` line count is a function of the substep length (`[1, 6, 278, 1]` over the partitions (5000,250), (5000,150), (5000,50) and (370,250)), because `step.rs` journals on the rising edge of the per-substep flag and tells the pilot on the rising edge of `held` (F.2); the remedy, gating the journal on `!was_held` and folding `held` into `state_hash`, changes what the hash covers; the three cadences agree only because they share one partition, and Q.7's blocked-cell memory is idempotent per cell so nothing built depends on the count. (b) The Withdraw stand is gated on full `order_reach_cm`, so a hurt machine with a live line to any classified hostile, the mesh's included, stands and duels and cannot rally while it holds one: bound it by a cornered radius or the engage band, after a playtest. (c) On the web every prefill leaves screen 0 for the scene screen, because that screen mounts the only composer; the TUI keeps the pilot on the cockpit; a screen-local input is a new surface. (d) The absolute substep lattice (plan question 10) is not built. (e) Whether the VPS has Node 22 (plan question 13). (2) Engine carry-forwards. X.6 deviation 13 is half closed: at the stock doctrine the plateau fixture still records seven `Blocked` cells, five on the cliff line, the 1 Hz decide cadence against a 15 m/s tank. A standing `Objective::Withdraw` re-enters `Withdraw` the tick after a rally, flipping to `Patrol` for one tick every `rally_ms` and spending `rallied`. `was` on a `Classified` notice is keyed on the book and not the row, so a once-numbered target re-acquired over the mesh says an `identified` line beside its acquisition. `DesignatorBook` is monotonic and folded row by row into every substep hash (a running digest is the remedy if an hour-long 300-unit match bites). `in_flight` is never cleared after an impact, and `observe_section_lost` is the first hashed mutation made during notice staging, landing in the following substep's hash with no sentence in `hash.rs`. `Arena::set_unit` recomputes the whole band walk on every heat write. `share_pass` pays one unbudgeted extra `in_range` scan per sharer and per mate in a pod-bearing match. `MIN_SUBSTEP_MS` can beat the one-cell bound above 20,000 cm/s on 10 m cells, where motion's guard refuses the jump. `would_overheat` never stages a weapon whose `heat_per_shot` exceeds the platform's whole ceiling. `first_step_is_open` reads only the heading's octant and its doc overstates parity with motion's step 4. The four config validators run with `[tacsim].enabled` off, and their table has no ladder-consistent `quiet_ms` row. `build_game` refuses, where C.2 skipped, an operator pack wearing a shipped id and version under another digest (deliberate). (3) The feed and the ring. The retain rule ignores `PanelFrameKind`, so a future marked `Delta` would evict its own base. `arena.map`'s retain marker is inert today, because a query reply is conn-scoped and never ring-recorded, which makes the clients' 2 s re-ask load-bearing; the mush-session resume paragraph does not mention superseded keyframes. The 256 KiB map bound is a test and not an enforcement, and both query handlers ignore `params` (the `window` parameter and `origin_cell` are reserved for the 10 km path). `PanelStore::forget` is `pub` and drops only `feeds`. `Arena::frame_scan_tables` is `pub` with test callers only. `scan_lines` can emit a 77-column run, and the `CONTACTS` column of four loses its separator at a four-character designator (unreachable for a player). (4) The TUI cockpit. Rows clip and do not wrap, so the scan and damage rows lose their tails in a 34-column pane; an 80-column terminal drops `weapons` and `contacts`, the only selectable pane; the legend still names a marker a later one overwrote; the map gets half the width surplus; the `0 cockpit` tab is sticky until `@notui`; `v` has no grid surface on the TUI; a throttled entry ask is never retried; a world with no tacsim earns one `Unknown query` line; auto-open sends one redundant ask; `docs/interface/02-implementation-plan.md` still says `MAX_SCREENS` is 9 (outside `docs/design`, left for a later cut). (5) The web cockpit. The zoom buttons' glyphs read against convention (their aria-labels are truthful); canvas labels are drawn only at 6 px a cell and up, so not at the default 128-cell view; an unknown scan band letter paints green; `applyMap` lets another match's late answer replace a held map for up to 2 s; the canvas repaints every terrain cell on every keyframe (65,536 rectangles at the 256-cell zoom) and nothing was measured on a full-size arena or a phone; the hello-armed wildcard waits out the latch on a match change inside the first 2 s; the fourteen notice phrasings are the client's own and unread in play; two comments are stale (`TERRAIN_COLOURS` added colours, not classes; the probe whitelists `cockpit` plus `focusComposer`). (6) Deploy. `deploy.sh`'s staleness check is an mtime heuristic over `web/src`: a rollback passes it, it ignores `web/index.html`, `package.json` and the vite config, and it cannot see a gateway binary built before the bundle; its README gives the wrong reason for the ship-the-bundle route, which fails safe; none of it has been executed. Q.3, Q.4 and Q.5 change the gateway half, so dev1 takes them with `systemctl restart dev1mush-gateway` and not a reload; Q.7's binary goes out before the rendered config, because `rally_ms` is a new key under `deny_unknown_fields`, and the template turns `ai_fill` on at the same render. (7) Small stale spots. The `cockpit.rs` module head still says ten verbs and its table lists neither `RANGE` nor `BEARING`; the in-game `cockpit_help` line for `STATUS` omits the callsign; the help never says a designator is case-folded; the help's `SCAN` example wraps at the brief's points, and its sentence that a watched section reads `destroyed` at once holds only for a pilot who already has a reading; `docs/help/simulator.md` states the rally without its arrival and dry conditions; `deliberation::request::BriefContact` carries no condition; Q.8's three known limits (the legged cell cost, the default-weapon rule, AI pods on the authored type) are stated in the help and not fixed. (8) Load-sensitive suites, all green in the sweep and in the closeout gate: `observer_rule` failed once under a builder's or fixer's parallel load at Q.3 (its shooting test) and at Q.4 and Q.7 (`nothing_about_an_undetected_unit_crosses_on_any_surface`); `arena_story_split` once at Q.1; `sim_reflex::with_ai_fill_on_the_default_size_is_the_larger_side` once at Q.8, identically with the change stashed; the cockpit owner-ruling test races a real 3 s provisional window because `classify_after_ms` is content; `sim_weapons`, `arena_story` and `observer_rule` each carry a new timing assumption that fails loudly; `BA` and `X1` are two-character needles in the leak scans, scoped to windows with no echo but more collision-prone than sixteen digits. (9) Test strength. `Hit.` with no held contact is pinned at `notice_line` level only, and `arena_bridge` asserts no survival of `scan_tables`, `condition` or the notice `section` key through emission. `keeping_destroyed` is in no cross-tempo run; `EndReason::Spent` has no cross-tempo test; the second reflex log has no re-engage leg, infers the stand from a `fired` line and lacks the liveness guard. Untested: the `avoid` clause of `first_step_is_open`; the two centre-wide abort sentences, with `NobodyPlaying` unreachable; a stop leaving `carry.turn` alone, a mate exactly at the share reach, and a reversing or westward unit (the Euclidean floor moves its first centimetre one substep early, bounded at 1 cm and partition-independent); the `None` pane fallback of the TUI map; the TUI degrade order behaviourally; two coincident markers, the palette's cover of every terrain glyph and the canvas painting on the web; the full-canonical-id and short-name-collision paths of `resolve_unit_type` and `type_word`; a path from `TacsimConfig` through `verify_sources` to `build_fielded`. The SSH lock witness proves the alias in one branch of its race and its `No such contact.` assertion is unreachable; `sim_solo.rs` makes no cold-platform claim; `two_locks_in_one_pulse_each_name_their_own_contact` no longer name-checks its second line; the TUI observer test does not assert the absence of the pilot name, the team word or a non-`@` glyph before detection, and carries a dead `Mapheading` alternative; the shipped-map round trip decodes with a private copy of `expand_rows`, and the cockpit query's headline equality re-runs the handler's own body; the held-billet leg of the `refit_unit` test is structural, its missing-definition refusal is matched by a loose substring, and two Q.8 assertions are partly tautological. Several final gates' clippy steps were cache hits on a tree already linted. Q.2's 961-byte fall in `ledger_bytes` was attributed to the changed hash being journalled as a variable-width decimal and nobody confirmed it. (10) Two tooling traps for the next briefs: a `\uXXXX` escape written through a JSON tool argument lands as a raw control byte that only `git diff --numstat` reveals, and a `git archive` export built into the repo's own `target/` silently overwrote the release `tacsim`, so give such an export its own `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`. **The owner's rulings** (2026-09-20, on the plan's thirteen questions): "new contact X1 until identified is fine, but if someone locks X1 and it becomes BA then their lock should continue since it is the same contact ... agreed on the rest". Every default of the plan therefore stands. (1) Provisional `X<n>` until classified, then team and slot, `designate_on_detection` off; the lock-continues clause needed no code, because the arena only ever receives a `ContactId`, and Q.6 pins it at arena, cockpit and SSH level. (2) A slot designator plus the public board names the pilot behind a classified contact: accepted, the board is not blanked, and doc 16 §5 says so. (3) A locked contact's range to 10 m, every other's to 50 m. (4) `SCAN` reads a classified contact inside half the effective reach, green at two thirds and yellow at one third, three `[tacsim.sensors]` keys. (5) The section struck is named only for an identified contact. (6) The cockpit on digit 0 in both clients, opened once per match. (7) Surveyed ground: the terrain is every panel client's from the start, the units are not, and the line client's `VIEW` stays 78 by 20. (8) With `ai_fill` on one player alone gets a match, `SIM AI <n>` sizes it, doc 16's minimum side becomes one, and the dev boxes turn the fill on. (9) A hurt machine backs off, shoots back if cornered and returns once after 20 quiet seconds, a released tank does the same, and both sides running dry is a draw. (10) No absolute substep lattice in cut Q. (11) The five extension units join the fielded catalog and `SIM UNIT` offers them to any pilot. (12) Arena notices stay `Sys` lines. (13) The SPA is built on the box, and `deploy.sh` refuses a stale bundle either way. |

the state lives on the `MatchRecord` and the doc comment says so) a `Player` reads the side of `centre.slot_of_pilot(actor)` in full and every other side as `{index} ({callsign}): {pilot name}` alone, a slotless player reads both reduced, `Staff` (`staff_status_rows`, so `@arena/status`) and every other state read the whole board byte for byte; a bare `Off` frame leaves it. `cockpit::power_of` (now `pub`) answers `Power::Starting` for `Off`-or-no-frame plus a live hint; `startup` reads it first (`Power is already online.` / `POWER_STARTING` / forward, then stamp `now + ceil(quiet_ms/1000) + 1`, fallback `HINT_FALLBACK_S` 6) and stamps only after `forward_intent` succeeds. `range_m_rounded` floors at one step. `Frame.sight_cm` (nominal `max_range_cells × cell_cm`, `0` with no sensor, compared in `same_as`), `cockpit::contact_band(range_cm, bands_cm, sight_cm)` (`beyond` past the last band, else thirds of `min(last band, sight)`, the last band alone when the sight is `0`) read by `frame_contacts`, so `CONTACTS`, the frame's `band` and the acquisition notice move together: 200 m / 400 m on the stock tank; `range_band` untouched and re-documented as the commander's word. `lobby::ai(None)` and `lobby::unit_type(None)` answer anybody in the centre in any state (` Yours:` only with a slot), the set refuses a non-lead with the new `SimError::NotLeadForAi` (`Only a side's first pilot or staff can set the AI fill.`). `ArenaOut::Frames` gains `now_ms` and may be empty; `Task::publish` sends it when a frame exists or `now_ms.div_euclid(1000)` differs from `last_clock_s`, still `try_send`, the seam still eight messages; `MatchMirror.now_ms` (`max` in `on_frames`, raised to `end.t_ms` in `on_ended`); the board prints ` arena time m:ss` through the shared `lobby::clock_text` and `@arena/status` keeps `arena {n} ms` and gains `clock m:ss`. `lobby::claim` says `the machines take the pods nobody claims` when `ai_fill` is on and the old sentence byte for byte when off. `cockpit::own_scope` (`hostile` / `unidentified` / `clear`) is `STATUS`'s third line (`; scope clear`, `; scope holds an unidentified contact`, `; scope holds a hostile`) and the frame key `scope`, beside the new `sight_m`; `tempo` stays. `BoardReader` and `clock_text` re-exported from `match_`. Shared scene given the shipped `sight_cm: 60_000` and re-blessed from one scene: `cockpit_frame.json`, `view_frame.json`, `web_view_model.txt`, `tui_cockpit_120x34.txt`, plus the one inline pin in `cockpit.test.ts` and a README paragraph. Help: `cockpit.md`'s band paragraph as scope advice and `STATUS`'s row, `simulator.md`'s `SIM STATUS`, `SIM AI`, `SIM UNIT` rows and the standing paragraph. Tests: `match_lifecycle.rs` four `d1_` (four readers over `Selection`, `Boarding`, `Running` and a stop in flight with the record `Stopping`; the two bare reads; the claim line both ways; `clock_text`'s edges), `cockpit.rs` seven (`contact_band`'s table with the shipped block and the floor; the frame keys and `own_scope`; the empty weapon list through the real egress seam via `Rig::push_frame`; the hint's ends; `untrack`'s sweep on a pilot with no remembered frame; the three scope sentences through the verb; the other side's row byte-identical across an `EXIT`), `arena_task.rs` three on tokio's paused clock (the clause at pinned 500 / 1,000 / 5,000 ms periods, the shipped config at no more than 1,050 ms between clocks, a frame never suppressed), `observer_rule.rs` one over real SSH from inside the pod, `sim_lobby.rs` three (`boot_with(tag, ai_fill)`). `crates/tacsim/Cargo.toml` gains a dev-only `tokio` `test-util` edge. No new verb, state slot, namespace, pack or digest pin; panic budget unmoved; no shipped client file changed. **D.2** (`1616fcc4`): Refusals reach the pilot. `Arena::step`'s phase-2 intent loop stages `FrameNotice::Refused` through the existing `stage_refusal` when the new const fn `Arena::phase_two_tells(&IntentKind, Refusal)` admits it: `Wreck`, `MotionDisabled`, `QueueFull` and `PowerNotOnline`, never an `IntentKind::Order`, and silent for `Epoch`, `Channel`, `Unknown`, `Linkdead` and `NotReflex`. The order word is `IntentKind::as_str` in both phases; phase 4's three literals `"startup"`, `"lock"` and `"fire"` are gone, and `weapon` is `Some` only on `Fire`. `Frame` gains `wreck` and `immobile` (off `UnitLimits`, both in `Frame::same_as`), and `status_frame` gains top-level `"wreck"` and `"immobile"`. The cockpit gains `DESTROYED`, `IMMOBILE`, `OWN_DESTROYED`, `OWN_DISABLED` and `FRIENDLY_MARK`, `CockpitError::{Destroyed, Immobile, AmbiguousWeapon, Refused}`, and the private `require_alive` and `require_mobile`. `require_alive` runs first in `startup`, `heading`, `speed`, `lock` and `fire`, before `resolve_designator`, `choose_weapon` and the power check; `require_mobile` runs on the two motion verbs; `IMMOBILE` is pinned byte for byte to `refusal_line`'s `MotionDisabled`. `NoticeKind`/`FrameNotice::{StruckSpent{section}, OwnDestroyed, OwnDisabled}` have the wire words `struck_spent`, `destroyed` and `disabled`. `EventKind::UnitDestroyed` stages `OwnDestroyed` to the victim after the gunner's `Kill`, and a new `EventKind::UnitDisabled` arm stages `OwnDisabled`; both are fieldless and answer `None` from every accessor. `STATUS` line 2 gains `, DESTROYED`. `FrameNotice` gains `cause()`, `refusal()`, `order()`, `weapon()`, `detail()` and `spent()`, and `Hit` gains `spent: bool`. The notice object gains `cause`, `refusal`, `order`, `weapon`, `detail` (`{"secs"}` or `{"heat","max"}` or null), `friendly` and `spent`. `Arena::spent_hits: BTreeSet<u64>` is filled in `apply_hit`'s empty-plan branch, drained first thing in the `Hit` arm of `stage_pilot_notice` in the same substep, and is UNHASHED. `Arena::spent_said: BTreeMap<(ObjId, ObjId, SmolStr), i64>` is HASHED, folded in `state_hash` after the units as the length then told, target, section bytes and stamp per entry. It is written through the private `Arena::say_spent` whether or not anybody is seated, and pruned only on touch inside the spent branch against the event's own timestamp. `pub const SPENT_NOTICE_MS: i64 = 5_000`. The classified gunner is keyed `(gunner, target, section)` and reads `Hit on BA: turret, already destroyed.`; the victim is keyed `(victim, victim, section)` and reads `Hit on the turret, which is already destroyed.` in place of `Struck`; the unidentified or unholding gunner has no key and reads today's line every round. `hash.rs`'s head is renumbered to ten items with one paragraph for each table. `Arena::resolve_fire` refuses `Overheated` only when `heat.per_shot(weapon) > 0`; `reflex::would_overheat` answers false for a zero-heat weapon before the ceiling test; `FrameWeapon.heat_per_shot` (in `same_as`, `"heat_per_shot"` on the wire, `WeaponRow.heat_per_shot`, 0 from `weapons_of`) lets `cockpit::fire` skip the heat refusal for a cold gun. `pub fn order_refused(order, weapon, said)` is the one shape, reached through `refusal_line` and the private `fire_refused`, so every `FIRE` refusal, local or arena-side and `PowerStarting` included, reads `FIRE <weapon> refused. ...`; `NoSuchWeapon` stays bare, and `LOCK` and `STARTUP` are unchanged. `pub fn weapon_initials`, `pub fn weapon_short_of` and `WeaponRow::render(&self, rows)` open `machine_gun (mg)` where the short name is unshared. `choose_weapon` tries exact id, then short name, then prefix, each step in full and none fallen through on ambiguity, answering `` `m` could be machine_gun or missile_gun; say more. ``. `(FRIENDLY)` appears on the `LOCK` echo (off `ContactRow::friendly`), on `lock_line` and on the hit (off the private `friendly_mark`), never on an `unknown` row and never as a gate. Both panels print `scope <word>` where they printed `tempo` (`render_cockpit_status_rows`, `statusLines`), and `CockpitFrame` gains `scope`, `wreck` and `immobile`; `tempo` stays decoded with no reader. Web `CockpitNotice` gains seven fields plus `CockpitNoticeDetail`, along with `REFUSAL_WORDS`, `CAUSE_WORDS`, `opened` and `detailWords`, and `noticeLine` composes from fields and never from `text`. The module head, the verb table and `COCKPIT_VERBS` agree (twelve selectors plus `BOARD` and `EXIT`), and the `STATUS` help names the callsign and scope. `docs/help/cockpit.md` gains `FIRE mg`, the prefix and the ambiguity, `SCAN`'s real precondition, case folding, the movement-signature and facet paragraphs, the friendly mark, both spent lines, the rewritten refusal section, and a new "When your tank is out of it" section. Tests added: ten in-source in `step.rs`, one in `hash.rs`, one in `reflex.rs` and six in `cockpit.rs`. `resolution.rs` adds the mandatory three-cadence run `d2_the_refusals_the_wreck_and_the_spent_section_are_one_list_at_three_cadences` (three tanks; one event list, one hash sequence and one ordered notice list per pilot; the `destroyed` line once, three `Wreck` refusals, exactly one `queue_full` from a nine-`LOCK` beat, `spent_rounds == 6` and `said == 5` at both readers) and the unidentified-gunner parity run. `extensions.rs` adds the zero-heat three-cadence run and a reflex-held cooked laser tank whose ledger fires only `machine_gun`. `tests/cockpit.rs` adds seven tests over the real world, bridge and verbs, including the cockpit's own zero-heat pre-check, the `LOCK` echo's friendly mark, and D.1's hint cleared end to end by a refused `STARTUP`. `observer_rule.rs` adds the victim's three lines; `sim_weapons.rs` adds `machine_gun (mg)` and `FIRE mg` over SSH; vitest gains the refusal, cause, friendly and spent tables plus hostile values for every new decode path. Harness: every simulation counter reproduces D.1's, `frames_per_pulse_mean` 21.35 is unchanged, engaged p99 is 7.4 to 8.0 ms over three runs, and `ledger_bytes` moved 1451475 to 1450389. No draw label, verb or pack was added, no proto type moved, and the panic budget is unchanged. **D.3** (`3c84b493`): The lifecycle's ends. One pre-`Running` funnel, private `lobby::call_off(ctx, why)` = `release(Aborted, Abort)` then `reset_stock_tanks` then `announce`, taken by `abort`'s `Selection`/`Boarding` arm, `settle_boarding`'s and `board_deadline`'s empty arms and BOTH of `run`'s refusal arms (`run` split into `run` + private `ready() -> Result<Ready, SimError>`, so `Unavailable`, `NobodyPlaying` and `EmptySide` call the lobby off with `Match {id} could not start: {error}. The pods are free again.` and still return the `Err`), so a `SIM UNIT` refit never outlives the lobby that chose it; one writer of `Idle`, private `to_idle` (`release_rows(Idle, None)` + `Centre::default()`), called by `reset` and by the last `leave`; `largest_side` clamped to `1..=PODS_PER_SIDE`. `StopReason::{Concede { by }, Unattended}` and `EndReason::{Conceded { winner }, Unattended}` (wire `{"conceded":{"winner":"blue"}}` and `"unattended"`), both journalled by `Arena::stopped` as `match_ended`, both `MatchState::Ended` with cells committed, outcome the winner's word and `timeout`, result `reason` words `conceded` and `unattended`, board lines `— {winner} wins: {loser} conceded` and `— timeout: every pilot had left`. `SIM CONCEDE`, the eighth selector (`lobby::concede`, re-exported `sim_concede`): layer, room, `Running`, a slot, the side's lead (`SimError::NotLeadToConcede`), no staff case, through `match_::abort`, its sentence said only while the centre is still `Running` after the funnel returns (a match whose task is gone is finished on the spot as an abort and the concede is not said over it). `[tacsim.match].unattended_ms` (300000; `0` off, else at least 10000, refused by name in `MatchConfig::validate`) in `tacsim::config`, `mush-config`, `config_ext` and all three shipped config files; `Centre.unattended_since_ms` and `unattended_warned`, stamped only by `slot_left` on a slotted `Running` centre whose every slot is `left`, cleared only by an accepted stop, `ControlRefusal::NoMatch` and `release_rows` (`Centre::clear_unattended`); `lobby::unattended_deadline` on `sim::board_tick` reads the key at the deadline, parks the `MatchRecord` in `Stopping` and says `pub fn unattended_line` on `Ok`, keeps the stamp and warns once on any other refusal. Reflex: the `Power::Off` and `Power::Starting` arms keep a `Withdraw`, so a released cold tank orders its own startup at its first tick, comes online `match.startup_ms` later and drives home still in `Withdraw`. `finish`'s check-in error arm: `release_seat`, one void retry, `UnitResult::lost`, the pose step, never a skipped unit; both restart-only log strings name `@arena/release`. `@arena/release <unit>` (sixth switch, staff): refused for a unit `ArenaRegistry` binds to a live match and for one with no marker, else void `check_in` + `release_seat` (now `pub(crate)`). `cockpit::waiting_to_start` answers `NOT_STARTED_YET` only in `Boarding` for a billet on a roster tank; `@arena/reset` has a third headline through `ResetOutcome::is_empty` (first caller of `ResetReport::is_empty`). `T-60` six of eight: non-staff `SIM ABORT` in `Selection` is `SimError::NothingToAbort`; the board drops ` — abort`/` — void` when the outcome repeats the state; `SPEED` names and enforces both ceilings off `TrackedRules::reverse_ceiling_cms` (`speed_ceilings` replaces `max_speed_cms`); `STATUS` brackets the fitted chassis via `fitted_bracket`, gated on `centre::tank_for_slot(..) == Some(unit)` and spelled by `lobby::type_word` (`pub(crate)`); bare `LOCK` is usage; `tell_room` takes an exclude list and `EXIT` excludes the actor. `legacy-handlers.toml`: the `sim` and `@arena` notes re-blessed, no stanza, baseline unmoved; help pages `simulator.md` and `cockpit.md`. Tests: `tests/arena.rs` (`Beat::at`/`Beat::stopping`, the stop ends, the mandatory cross-tempo stop test at 25000 ms, `an_order_at_the_epoch_the_purge_published_is_still_admitted`), `tests/reflex.rs` (the cold-release arc and its mandatory cross-tempo test), sixteen `d3_` tests in `tests/match_lifecycle.rs` with `Rig::{schema_of, release, tick_heard, strand, station_on, holds_billet, tasks, end_match_returning}`, `damaged_return`, `over_full`, three in `tests/cockpit.rs`, two SSH tests in `sim_lobby.rs`, one on the real clock in `sim_solo.rs` with a live-byte observer sweep, one in `observer_rule.rs` over the production sentence, `shipped_configs.rs` and the `config_ext` refusal rows; tacsim 767 tests; harness p99 7.9 ms against the 12 ms lens with every simulation counter reproducing D.2's. **D.4** (`aa2ae301`): What a contact is called. `B-20`: `stage_pilot_notice`'s `Detected` arm, on cancelling a held loss, compares the designator `held_losses` carried with `name_of` and stages `FrameNotice::Contact { kind: NoticeKind::Unidentified, class: Unknown, was: Some(old) }` only when the live row is still `Classification::Unknown` and the name moved. The wire word is `unidentified` and the sentence is `BA is unidentified again: X2.` Equal names stay silent, as does any row under `designate_on_detection`. A mesh share that hands the row back `Classified` inside the window also stays silent. `resolve_designator` is untouched, so `LOCK BA` is still the one byte-identical refusal. `B-21`: `ShadowContact.numbered` is `true` only at `sensor_pass`'s insert with the switch off, beside `designators.assign`. It is `false` in `share_pass`'s insert and in `ShadowContact::sighted`, and is never rewritten. `Classified`'s `was` reads it in place of the permanent book, so a mesh re-arrival is one `New contact BA` line, while a share that classifies an own-opened hold still says the rename. `T-19`: `Acquired` carries the live row's class. `B-22`: the private `Arena::detections` returns `Sweep { found, jam }` with the recovered fold, so `jamming_at` still runs once a pass and no perception counter moves; X.4's `jam_checks == 2` is re-pinned. `sensor_pass` derives `UnitState.receiver: Option<Receiver { budget, budget_max, reach_permille }>`, which is `None` for no fold, for a wholly recovered fold and for no pass. `destroy_unit` clears it silently beside `sensor`, because the wreck's `SensorPass` leaves the due heap. `EventKind::Receiver { unit, degraded }` (kind word `receiver`, no draw) is journalled on the `None`/`Some` transition only, at the pass's own timestamp, and stages `FrameNotice::Receiver { degraded }` with kinds `receiver_degraded` and `receiver_clear`. The sentences are `Receiver degraded: something is jamming the sight.` and `Receiver clear.` `Frame.receiver` is in `same_as`. `status_frame` gains a top-level `receiver` of `{budget, budget_max, reach_percent}` or `null`. `STATUS` gains `cockpit::receiver_line` after the heat line. `T-08`: `SectionDestroyed` and `UnitDestroyed` carry `by: Option<ObjId>` (wire `"by":<id>`, omitted when `None`). It is set from `resolve_impact`'s shot and threaded through the now five-argument `apply_consequence` and four-argument `destroy_unit`. The gunner's `TargetSectionLost` plus `observe_section_lost`, and `Kill`, read `by` alone. `in_flight` now serves `Miss` alone and is cleared at the end of the `Hit` and `Miss` arms. `T-20`: `cockpit::lock_range_step_m(age_ms)` is `age x PLOT_DRIFT_M_PER_S (17) / 1000` rounded up to ten and clamped to `LOCK_RANGE_STEP_M..=CONTACT_RANGE_STEP_M`. Both `range_line` and `status_frame`'s `lock_range_m` read it off the locked row's own `age_ms`. The locked metres are published once and never recomputed client-side. `state_hash` folds `receiver` per unit after the motion carries (a presence byte, two `u8`, one `u16`) and `numbered` as one byte per contact after the readings, both under the rule that persistent state deciding a notice is hashed. Tests: three in-source `step.rs` tests for `T-08` and one `hash.rs` probe; six in `perception.rs` (the classified flicker, the name-keeping flicker, the loss past the window, the share rename with the mesh one-liner and `T-19`, the mesh share inside the window, and the flicker at three cadences with its column); seven in `extensions.rs` (the edge, stacked pods, counters, filters, the wreck through `destroy_unit`, the frame that tells the pilot, and the bubble drive at three cadences), with `Q6Replay` now carrying per-pilot staged `FrameNotice` lists and a differing-pulses guard for every Q.6-style replay; the `by` duel at three cadences in `resolution.rs`; six in `tests/cockpit.rs`; and one observer-rule test. Goldens move on exactly one line (`Lock BA 430 m` to `440 m`) in the two TUI goldens and `web_view_model.txt`. `cockpit_frame.json` and `view_frame.json` move `lock_range_m` and gain `"receiver": null`. `docs/help/cockpit.md` gains the age-derived lock paragraph, the regression paragraph and the receiver paragraph. Harness: every simulation counter reproduces D.3's verbatim. Ledger bytes move from 1,450,389 to 1,452,761: `by` on 60 lines plus the decimal-width drift of the per-substep hash. Engaged p99 is 7.4 ms against the 12 ms lens. No verb, pack, config key, draw label, state slot or gate moved. **D.5** (`4952615c`): The way in. **The exit check and its seams.** `mush-core` gains `Registry::kernel_command_named(word) -> Option<&str>` beside `spec_of`. It returns the primary name of the entry indexed under exactly that word, case-folded, only when its module is `None` (the kernel's and the host's verbs), and never by prefix. `mush-content` gains `EntryApplier::check(ctx, pack, id, entry) -> Result<(), String>`, default `Ok(())`. `apply` runs it for every `Step::Create` and `Step::Update` entry, after the retire checks and before the irreversible half. It also gains `ApplyError::EntryRefused { entry, reason }`, whose `Display` is the reason alone. The atlas's `KindApplier::check` runs the private `check_exit_names` for kind `exit`. It refuses any `;` segment of any name, the display segment included, that `kernel_command_named` answers. The refusal reads `exit '{id}' uses '{segment}' as a name; that is the command '{command}', which works in every room, and an exit would swallow it`. It writes nothing and moves no latch. A module's verb is accepted on cut F.4's ruling, and the ruling is written into the doc comment. The alias `l` comes off five shipped exits: `exit.building_lounge`, `exit.hall_lounge` and `exit.bus_stop_lounge` in `neighborhood.ooc_area`, and `exit.limbo_forecourt` and `exit.lobby_breakroom` in `neighborhood.staff_lobby`. They keep `<L>`, the word and the compass words. **Room details.** `Registrar::detail_prefix(prefix)` is refused unless the text before the first `:` is an attribute namespace this module already claimed. It has two refusal sentences. The prefix lands in the private `ModuleTables.detail_prefixes`, de-duplicated and in registration order, read through `Registry::detail_prefixes()`. `mush_core::attr::fold_detail_keyword`, re-exported at the crate root, is the one fold: trim, ASCII lowercase, each run of spaces to one `_`. The atlas's `dress` and `look` both call it. `mush_atlas::module::DETAIL_PREFIX = "atlas:detail:"` is registered straight after `attr_namespace(ATTR_NS)`. `look <keyword>` tries `detail:<q>` on the room and then the looker as before. It then tries each registered prefix plus the folded keyword, room then looker, and otherwise gives the byte-identical `MatchErr::NoMatch`. With no prefix registered `look` is unchanged. **Content and versions.** The Simulator Selector's `desc` no longer claims `SIM START` needs a pilot on both sides, and its `details.screen` names `SIM CONCEDE`. `neighborhood.ooc_area` goes v5 to v6, digest `64f6cf88104b80c4bf9af2b013ec2cba089cf2e664f827a72f29184a9cc54691`. `neighborhood.staff_lobby` goes v3 to v4, requiring OOC v6, digest `8d791c31a7c81c794cb78529b2735e0c1ab360aea92aa8d03c3d571388056796`. `region.limbo` is unmoved. Both pins in `tests/starter.rs` were re-derived from the test's own output. `deploy/README.md` carries one paragraph on taking the pair by hand. **Help.** `docs/help/{wod,shadowrun,action}.md` are deleted, leaving 33 pages. `perception.md` is rewritten on the arena's contacts: `CONTACTS`, the `X2` to `BA` rename, `BA is unidentified again: X2.`, the `No such contact.` non-oracle, and `SCAN`'s two gates. `behavior.md`, `npc.md`, `intel.md`, `discord.md` and `tui.md` are corrected. **New contract tests in `mush-contract-tests`.** `tests/production_registry/mod.rs` exposes `production_composition()`. It composes `base_registry` plus `register_server_commands` plus `boot::game_modules`, the real root. `tests/help_commands.rs` is `T-45`. It checks the head word, and every `/switch`, of each inline code span and each fenced line in `docs/help/*.md` against the production registry. A fence delimiter is a line with exactly one triple-backtick run. Exceptions live in `help-commands-allowlist.toml`: 14 rows, each with a reason, and an unused row fails. `tests/exit_command_words.rs` has two tests: `the_shipped_exits_take_exactly_the_module_verbs_that_were_ruled_on` pins exactly `("exit.bus_stop_bus","board","board")` and `("exit.forecourt_bus","board","board")`. `no_shipped_exit_segment_is_a_word_the_kernel_or_the_host_owns`, with a `@boot` guard against vacuity. **Other tests added or changed.** `mush-atlas/tests/lot.rs`: `l` looks in all five rooms; three refused packs (`l`, `inv`, `home`) write nothing; `loo` and the module verb `waylay` are let through, with a positive module-verb assertion; details read back, with the bare `detail:` winning. `mush-atlas/tests/starter.rs`: the composed-world check over the three packs. `mush-atlas/tests/interior.rs`: `SHIPPED_VERSION` is 6, the v4 fixture is the literal 4, and the 4 to 6 upgrade proves a hand-placed `l` alias is dropped when the exit is remade. `mush-atlas/tests/window.rs`: the next version is read off the shipped bytes. `mush-core/tests/module_api.rs`: the prefix seam and its five refusals. `mush-core/tests/commands.rs`: `look` is unchanged with no prefix registered. `attr.rs`: the fold's table and a doctest. Both front-door walks gain an `l` step. Fixtures in `geometry`, `hydrate`, `validate` and `mush-worldgen` drop the kernel words `l` and `p` from their authored exits. **Gates.** `module-ns-owners`, the legacy-handler baseline, the panic budgets and the restriction lints are unmoved, and no CI inventory row is needed. No file under `crates/tacsim/` changed, so no harness run and no cross-tempo test is owed. `sim_cockpit` is untouched and green, so `board` still boards the bus on a fresh production world. **The sweep repair** (`5703862f`): the full sweep on `4952615c` found one real defect, D.5's own. `crates/mush-housing/tests/s2b_site.rs` still authored the exit `Parlour <P>;p;parlour`, and `p` is the kernel's alias for `page`, so D.5's new apply check refused the whole `neighborhood.block` fixture pack before its first write, no room was made, and the test panicked four frames from the cause. The `;p` segment is dropped, exactly as D.5 did in `mush-atlas/tests/validate.rs`, with a doc comment on `packs()` naming `check_exit_names` and 16c `B-11` so the alias is not put back; no assertion moved. (2) The check runs through a new `mush-content` hook, `EntryApplier::check`, with a new `ApplyError::EntryRefused`. The brief named neither, but this is the only place a refusal takes the whole pack before its first write. The error prints the reason alone because the sentence already names its entry. (3) The check runs on `Step::Create` and `Step::Update` entries only. An `Unchanged` exit is never re-examined, so an operator pack latched before D.5 is not refused until that entry itself changes. This is narrower than the brief's 'any exit entry', and kinder to a live world. (4) `kernel_command_named` folds with `to_lowercase()`, not ASCII-only folding, so that it agrees with the index `spec_of` reads. The two are identical for ASCII. (5) The fold collapses runs of spaces to one `_`, where the old writer emitted one `_` per space. No shipped keyword has a double space, so no attribute name and no digest moved for that reason. (6) A malformed exit entry passes `check` and is reported by `create_exit` as before. (7) `crates/mushd/tests/persistence.rs` was not changed, against the brief's prediction. This is a decision for the owner and a new 16c row. (8) Seven fixture files outside the brief's list changed because they authored kernel words (`l`, `p`) as exit aliases. Its v4 fixture is the literal 4, so the upgrade under test is 4 to 6. (10) The T-45 allowlist is keyed on the token alone, not on file and token. It reads inline spans and fenced blocks only, not the indented usage blocks most pages use, and checks only a span's head word, so an argument error such as `FIRE mg` is out of its reach although its head comment implies otherwise. The 14 rows are all prose capitals or front-end and gateway verbs, five real help errors were fixed, and nothing was allowlisted to avoid fixing it. It and T-45 share `tests/production_registry/mod.rs`, which composes `boot::game_modules`. `legacy_handler_allowlist.rs` keeps its own roster copy because its pinned baseline counts it. One predicate `Arena::contact_reads_dead(&self, held: &ShadowContact) -> bool` in `perception.rs`: `false` when `self.unit(held.target)` is `None` (the lookup is the first line, so gone is never dead), else `held.known_dead \|\| seen_condition.destroyed_mask() & target.fatal_sections != 0`; its **five readers** moved off `target.limits.wreck`: `hostile_in_weapons_range`, the `lost` edge in `decide` (which now resolves the hold once), `holds_a_hostile`, `best_hostile` (`reflex.rs`) and `contact_within_alert_range` (`perception.rs`), each still dropping a target whose unit has left the match at once; `reflex_has_line`, `fire_line`, the sensor pass's `sees_wrecks` rule, `resolve_lock`, `resolve_fire` and the impact's re-validation stay physics and say so. **B-40**: in `decide`'s `Withdraw` arm the rally guard is `holds_a_hostile \|\| !arrived \|\| dry \|\| ordered_home` where `ordered_home` is a live `Objective::Withdraw`, so `quiet_since_ms` stays `None` for the order's life and the existing expiry block returns a whole hull to `Patrol` at once and starts a hurt one's clock from that tick; `quiet_since_ms` and `rallied` docs say so. **B-41**: `Arena::follow` gains `order: Option<&Order>` (passed as `order.as_ref()`, the squad-filtered order `decide` decided under, not `row.order`); in its `Err` arm before `fall_back_to_waypoint`, when the order is `Objective::Advance { to }` and `row.goal == Some(to)`, `row.order = None` and `plan.expired = Some((squad, "advance"))`; `reflex_tick` then journals the existing `EventKind::OrderExpired { unit, squad, objective: "advance" }` at that tick's arena millisecond, counts `orders_expired` and calls `note_order_expired`, so the next `SquadSignal` carries `SignalReason::OrderExpired`; a stand-off cell, an entry cell or a patrol node that fails drops nothing; no new field, nothing new hashed, no new kind or reason, `arena_bridge::say_expired_orders` untouched; `EventKind::OrderExpired`, `Objective::Advance` and `StandingOrder` docs carry the second cause. **T-55**: `would_overheat` is `false` with no heat state, no heat cell or `per_shot == 0`, otherwise `unit.overheated()`; the `now + per_shot > max` line and its doc paragraph are gone, `engage_orders`' paragraph updated, the unit test's 50-of-60 row flipped to `(false, false)`. **T-56**: `SquadSignal::default_ttl_ms: u32` = `arena_cfg.deliberate_min_ms.clamp(MIN_TTL_MS, MAX_TTL_MS)` set in `take_deliberation_signals`; `SquadBrief::default_ttl_ms` copied by `from_signal` (no serde default, `deny_unknown_fields` kept); `order_of` reads `payload.ttl_ms.unwrap_or(brief.default_ttl_ms)` and `horizon_ms` stays `payload.horizon_ms.unwrap_or(ttl_ms).min(MAX_HORIZON_MS)`, so an order omitting both records 30,000 where it recorded 1,000; `horizon_ms` has no production reader in `src/arena` (two test literals); `user_prompt` does not print it and `order_tool` still requires `ttl_ms`. Tests: `tests/reflex.rs` eight `e2_b45_` (held to expiry after a `by: None` destroy, gone is not dead, a hull reading drops and a turret reading does not with `fatal_sections == 0b01` pinned, both tempo triggers read the hold, the rally clock starts at the kill and not at a death out of sight, a real `FIRE` kill written on the shooter's hold alone with a direct-sighting teammate waiting for expiry, and a mesh-only teammate whose row has `own_seen` None opening and staying `known_dead` false through the insert and update arms) plus two three-cadence replays (a meshed pair's kill under a withdraw order that expires mid-run; an unreachable `Advance` and a hot gun at fifty of sixty, one `order_expired` under 2,000 ms and exactly one `fired`), with fixtures `with_mesh`, `killable`, `two_sectioned`, `pistol`, `curtain`, `bystander`; `tests/deliberation.rs` five `e2_b41_` (two failures and two lines over twenty seconds of a ten-minute order with the same cell re-sent costing one more each, a reachable cell obeyed to arrival, doctrine back the tick after, review E-04's pin that an `Engage` stand-off cell and a `Withdraw` entry cell drop nothing, and a cell sealed only by a remembered `Occupied` stop dropping the order as the recorded cost), two `e2_b40_`, one `e2_t56_`, with `field_placed`, `definition_painted`, `sealed_pocket`, `run_counted`, `park`; `request.rs` two `e2_t56_` unit tests; `hash.rs` one probe; `tests/perception.rs`'s wreck test renamed `a_contact_on_a_wreck_holds_the_alert_tempo_until_its_holder_knows`; `tests/extensions.rs`'s cold-gun test re-fixtured to `per_s = 0` with `heat_now == heat_max` pinned. Harness under `TACSIM_ASSERT=1`: p50 1.049 / p95 6.917 / p99 7.162 / max 7.413 ms against E.1's 7.363; 767 fired, 691 hits, 76 misses, 9 units destroyed, reflex traces 244, path cells 110,811, path failures 0, `alert_range_cm` 362,000; no key, pack, verb, notice, frame field, ledger kind, draw label or gate moved. **E.3** (`c3852a0c`): The traffic hold, braking for the goal, least-recent eviction and the routeless pre-pass. `arena/state.rs`: `pub struct OccupiedHold { pub cell: (u16, u16), pub since_ms: i64 }` (`Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq`, no serde; `cell` is the cell the crossing was refused, `since_ms` the arena millisecond the substep that opened it ended at) and `UnitState::occupied_hold: Option<OccupiedHold>`, `None` at check-out, one writer `motion::integrate`, cleared by an accepted crossing, a substep that moved inside its cell, a refusal of a different cell or any other cause, `desired_speed_cms == 0` (at the top of `advance` and again inside the arm, after the maturity test), and `accelerate`'s wreck / dead-engine / cold-power return; a substep that covered no whole centimetre leaves it alone. `hash.rs`: folded immediately after `grade_pct` as a presence byte, `cell.0`, `cell.1` (`u16` LE) and `since_ms` (`i64` LE), the numbered list renumbered 13/14/15, the argument written at the head. `motion.rs`: `integrate(unit, dt_ms, terrain, index, end_ms: i64, occupied_stop_ms: u32)` and `advance` likewise, two call sites (`Arena::substep` with `end_ms = now_ms + dt_ms` read before the motion borrow, and the test helper `step_under`); the `Occupied` arm in order: mature (`occupied_stop_ms == 0`, or a hold naming `to` with `end_ms - since_ms >= occupied_stop_ms`) then `stop_at_boundary` and `occupied_hold = None`; else `desired_speed_cms == 0` then clear and hold; else open `OccupiedHold { cell: to, since_ms: end_ms }` if the hold does not already name `to`, and hold. New `hold_at_boundary` clamps both axes through the shared `clamp_into_cell` (extracted from `stop_at_boundary`), zeroes `carry.east` and `carry.north`, keeps `speed_cms` and `carry.speed`, leaves `blocked` false and returns `MotionOutcome::default()`, so no `blocked` line, no notice, no `note_blocked` and `held` untouched; its doc records the decided frame (kept speed, no `HELD`) and the diagonal slide. Impassable, `Edge` and `TooSteep` stop at once and clear the hold. `integrate`'s and `UnitState::held`'s docs now say the controller learns from the `blocked` event. `step.rs`: `Frame::speed_cms`'s doc records what a pilot reads during a hold; `Arena::substep` calls `self.hold_routeless(reflex)` before phase (a). `reflex.rs`: `pub fn Arena::hold_routeless(&mut self, counters: &mut ReflexCounters)` walks `self.reflex.rows()` (a `BTreeMap`, unit-id order) skipping a non-empty route, no goal, a missing unit, `controller != Reflex`, `power != Online`, `desired_speed_cms == 0`, a cell off the window, and `first_step_is_open(unit, here, pose.heading_mdeg, &row.avoid)` true; the surviving ids are collected first, then each is ordered `IntentKind::Speed { cms: 0 }` through `apply_intent` at `epochs.current(id, Channel)` with `seq: 1`, counted in `ReflexCounters::intents`, draws nothing and journals nothing. Private `braking_speed_cms(unit, goal, cell_cm, decide_ms) -> i32` replaces `follow`'s flat `max_speed_cms`: `brake == 0` gives the ceiling; else planar `range_between` to the goal cell centre, `d = range - ARRIVE_CELLS x cell_cm`, `d_eff = d - abs(speed_cms) x decide_ms / 1000`, `min(max_speed_cms, isqrt(2 x brake x d_eff))`, all `u64` saturating; the arrive-window `speed 0` and `Engage`'s own `speed 0` untouched. `note_blocked` evicts `min_by_key((at_ms, index))` when full; `REFLEX_AVOID_MAX` and `ReflexUnit::avoid` docs say least recently stopped at. Config: `[tacsim.arena].occupied_stop_ms`, `u32`, default 2,000, `between(0, 60_000)` refusing as `[tacsim.arena].occupied_stop_ms must be 0 to 60000 (it is 60001)`, 0 documented as the pre-cut rule; mirrored on `ArenaConfig`, `TacsimArenaCfg` (doc table, struct, `Default`, both parse tests), `config_ext` (mapper, round-trip at 1500, refusal row 60001), `shipped_configs.rs` (2,000 on both rendered instances and the example), `mushd.toml.example`, `deploy/instance/mushd.toml.tmpl` and `deploy/dev2/mushd.toml` with the brief's comment verbatim. `docs/help/cockpit.md`: the sentence `Another unit in your way does not stop you at once: the tank holds against it at the speed it had and goes on if the way clears; only when it has not cleared in two seconds does the tank stop and the line arrive.` after `...a bank is never a one-way door.`, no existing sentence changed.
